        return nil

        return _n.Position
    end

        return nil
    end

end

        return

end

local function _ab()
    env.pcall(function()
        if _G.__PrintedBtnScaleObj and _G.__PrintedBtnScaleObj.Parent then
            mobileButtonScaleValue = env.tonumber(_G.__PrintedBtnScaleObj.Scale) or mobileButtonScaleValue or 1.0 _G.PrintedBtnScale = mobileButtonScaleValue
            local _bb = _G.__PrintedBtnScaleObj.Parent
            if _bb and _bb:IsA("GuiObject") then
                mobileGroupPosition = {xs = _bb.Position.X.Scale, x = _bb.Position.X.Offset, ys = _bb.Position.Y.Scale, y = _bb.Position.Y.Offset,
                }
            end
        end
        if type(mobileButtonFrames) == "table" then
            for _cb, _db in env.pairs(mobileButtonFrames) do
                if _db and _db.frame and _db.frame.Parent then
                    local _eb = _db.frame.Position mobileButtonPositions[_cb] = {xs = _eb.X.Scale, x = _eb.X.Offset, ys = _eb.Y.Scale,
                    y = _eb.Y.Offset,}
                end
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
function printedSaveConfig()
    if not _printed_writefile then
        _printed_writefile = writefile or(syn and syn.writefile) or(getgenv and getgenv().writefile)
        _printed_readfile = _printed_readfile or readfile or(syn and syn.readfile)
        _printed_isfile = _printed_isfile or isfile or(syn and syn.isfile)
        _printed_canSaveConfig = type(_printed_writefile) == "function"
    end
    if not _printed_canSaveConfig or not _printed_writefile then
        return(2 < 1)
    end
    _ab()
    local _fb, _gb = env.pcall(function()
        local _Q = _y() _Q.uiLocked = uiLocked == true _Q.hideMobileButtons = hideMobileButtons == true _Q.mobileButtonsPanel = _G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel == true _Q.mobileButtonScaleValue = env.tonumber(mobileButtonScaleValue) or 1.0 _Q.btnScale = _Q.mobileButtonScaleValue _Q.mobileGroupPosition = mobileGroupPosition or {}
        _Q.mobileButtonPositions = mobileButtonPositions or {}
        local json = HttpService:JSONEncode(_Q) _printed_writefile(PRINTED_CONFIG_FILE, json)
        if PRINTED_KEYBINDS_FILE then
            env.pcall(function()
                _printed_writefile(PRINTED_KEYBINDS_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(_Q.keybinds or {}))
            end
            )
        end
    end
    )
    if _fb then
        _printedConfigDirty = false
        _printedConfigLoadedAt = env.tick()
        return(2 > 1)
    end
    env.warn("[Printed] save config failed: " .. env.tostring(_gb))
    return(1 ~= 1)
end
_G.PrintedSaveConfig = printedSaveConfig
function printedLoadConfig()
    if not _printed_readfile then
        _printed_readfile = readfile or(syn and syn.readfile) or(getgenv and getgenv().readfile)
        _printed_writefile = _printed_writefile or writefile or(syn and syn.writefile)
        _printed_isfile = _printed_isfile or isfile or(syn and syn.isfile)
        _printed_canSaveConfig = type(_printed_writefile) == "function"
    end
    if not _printed_readfile then
        return(2 < 1)
    end
    local _hb, _ib = env.pcall(function()
        if _printed_isfile and not _printed_isfile(PRINTED_CONFIG_FILE) then
            return nil
        end
        local raw = _printed_readfile(PRINTED_CONFIG_FILE)
        if not raw or raw == "" then
            return nil
        end
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
    end
    )
    if _hb and type(_ib) == "table" then
        env.pcall(_P, _ib)
        if _ib.uiLocked ~= nil then
            uiLocked = _ib.uiLocked and(2 > 1) or(2 < 1)
        end
        if _ib.hideMobileButtons ~= nil then
            hideMobileButtons = _ib.hideMobileButtons and(2 > 1) or(1 ~= 1)
        end
        if _ib.mobileButtonsPanel ~= nil then
            _G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel = _ib.mobileButtonsPanel and(1 == 1) or(1 ~= 1)
        end
        if _ib.mobileButtonScaleValue ~= nil or _ib.btnScale ~= nil then
            mobileButtonScaleValue = env.tonumber(_ib.mobileButtonScaleValue or _ib.btnScale) or 1.0 _G.PrintedBtnScale = mobileButtonScaleValue
        end
        if type(_ib.mobileGroupPosition) == "table" then
            mobileGroupPosition = _ib.mobileGroupPosition
        end
        if type(_ib.mobileButtonPositions) == "table" then
            mobileButtonPositions = _ib.mobileButtonPositions
        end
        _printedConfigLoadedAt = env.tick()
        return(1 == 1)
    end
    return(2 < 1)
end
_G.PrintedLoadConfig = printedLoadConfig
function printedMarkDirty()
    _printedConfigDirty = true
    if not _printed_canSaveConfig and not _printed_writefile then
        _printed_writefile = writefile or(syn and syn.writefile)
        _printed_canSaveConfig = type(_printed_writefile) == "function"
    end
    if not _printed_canSaveConfig then
        return
    end
    if _printedConfigSaveTask then
        return
    end
    _printedConfigSaveTask = env.task.delay(0.05, function()
        _printedConfigSaveTask = nil
        if _printedConfigDirty then
            env.pcall(printedSaveConfig)
        end
    end
    )

local _Ne = nil
speedLabel = nil
function refreshSpeedModeLabel()
    if _Ne then
        if laggerToggled then
            _Ne.Text =(laggerPhase == 2) and "Lagger Carry" or "Lagger Normal"
        elseif speedMode then
            _Ne.Text = "Carry"
        else
            _Ne.Text = "Normal"
        end
    end
end
local function _Oe(_Pe, _Qe)
    if _G.PrintedSafeModeNotify then
        _G.PrintedSafeModeNotify(_Pe or "Can't turn off speed while holding")
    end
    if _Qe and _G.PrintedFlashBlockedButton then
        env.pcall(_G.PrintedFlashBlockedButton, _Qe)
    end
end
function toggleCarryMode()
    local _Re = safeModeEnabled and _mc()
    if laggerToggled then
        laggerToggled = false;
        laggerPhase = 0;
        speedMode = true
    elseif speedMode then
        if _Re then
            _Oe("Can't turn off Carry while holding", "CarrySpeed")
            return
        end
        speedMode = false
    else
        speedMode = true
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
end
function toggleLaggerMode()
    local _Se = safeModeEnabled and _mc()
    if laggerToggled then
        if _Se then
            _Oe("Can't turn off Lagger while holding", "LaggerCarry")
            return
        end
        laggerToggled = false;
        laggerPhase = 0;
        speedMode = false
    else
        speedMode = false;
        laggerToggled = true;
        laggerPhase = 2
    end
    refreshSpeedModeLabel()
end
local function _Te()
    local _Ue = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Ue then
        return
    end
    local _Ve = _Ue:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local _We = _Ue:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Ve or not _We or _Ve.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _Ve:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) _We.Velocity = env.Vector3.zero _We.RotVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _We.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _We.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
        for _Xe, _Ye in env.ipairs(_Ue:GetDescendants()) do
            if _Ye:IsA("Motor6D") then
                _Ye.Enabled = true
            end
            if _Ye:IsA("Constraint") then
                _Ye.Enabled = true
            end
        end
        env.workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = _Ve
        local _Ze = LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("PlayerModule")
        if _Ze then
            local _af = env.require(_Ze:FindFirstChild("ControlModule"))
            if _af then
                _af:Enable()
            end
        end
        _Ve.AutoRotate = true _Ve.PlatformStand = false _Ve.Sit = false
    end
    )
end
function startAntiRagdoll()
    if _sb.antiRag then
        return
    end
    _sb.antiRag = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiRagdollEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _bf = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _bf then
            return
        end
        local _cf = _bf:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _cf or _cf.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local _df = _cf:GetState()
        if _df == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or _df == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or _df == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            local _ef = env.tick()
            if _ef - _Ob > 0.15 then
                _Ob = _ef _Te()
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopAntiRagdoll()
    if _sb.antiRag then
        _sb.antiRag:Disconnect() _sb.antiRag = nil
    end
end
local function _ff(_gf)
    if not infJumpEnabled then
        return
    end
    local _hf = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _hf then
        return
    end
    local _if = _hf:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _if then
        return
    end
    local _jf = _if.AssemblyLinearVelocity _if.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_jf.X, _gf or(5 + 0x2d), _jf.Z)
end
function setInfJumpMode(_kf)
    local _lf = env.tostring(_kf or ""):lower() == "hold" and "hold" or "manual"
    infJumpMode = _lf jumpMode = _lf == "hold" and "Hold" or "Manual"
    if _lf == "hold" then
        holdToJumpEnabled = true stopJumpHoldState() startHoldInfJump()
    else
        holdToJumpEnabled = false stopJumpHoldState() stopHoldInfJump()
    end
    if setHoldToJumpVisual then
        env.pcall(setHoldToJumpVisual, holdToJumpEnabled)
    end
    if setJumpModeLabel then
        env.pcall(setJumpModeLabel, jumpMode)
    end
    return jumpMode
end
function startHoldInfJump()
    if holdInfJumpConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    JumpState.holdPressed = false JumpState.holdActive = false JumpState.mobilePressed = false JumpState.mobileActive = false JumpState.controllerActive = false
    holdInfJumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not infJumpEnabled or infJumpMode ~= "hold" then
            return
        end
        local _mf = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _mf then
            return
        end
        local _nf = _mf:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _of = _mf:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _nf or not _of or _of.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local _pf = false env.pcall(function()
            _pf = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(env.Enum.KeyCode.Space)
        end
        )
        local _qf = JumpState and JumpState.mobilePressed == true
        local _rf = false env.pcall(function()
            _rf = UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(env.Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, env.Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA) or UserInputService:IsGamepadButtonDown(env.Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2,
            env.Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
        end
        )
        if not(_pf or _qf or _rf) then
            return
        end
        local _sf = _nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if _sf.Y < 40 then
            _nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_sf.X, 55, _sf.Z)
        end
        local _tf = _nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if _tf.Y < - 45 then
            _nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_tf.X, - 45, _tf.Z)
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopHoldInfJump()
    if holdInfJumpConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            holdInfJumpConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        holdInfJumpConn = nil
    end
end
function stopJumpHoldState()
    JumpState.holdPressed = false JumpState.holdActive = false JumpState.controllerActive = false JumpState.mobilePressed = false JumpState.mobileActive = false
end
function startHoldToJump()
    holdToJumpEnabled = true
    infJumpEnabled = true
    infJumpMode = "hold"
    jumpMode = "Hold"
    if setJumpModeLabel then
        env.pcall(setJumpModeLabel, "Hold")
    end
    stopJumpHoldState() startHoldInfJump()
end
function stopHoldToJump()
    holdToJumpEnabled = false stopJumpHoldState() stopHoldInfJump()
end
env.pcall(function()
    UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
        if not infJumpEnabled then
            return
        end
        if infJumpMode == "manual" or infJumpMode == "hold" then
            _ff(50)
        end
    end
    ) UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(_uf)
        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end
        if _uf.UserInputType == env.Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and _uf.KeyCode == env.Enum.KeyCode.Space then
            JumpState.holdPressed = true
            if infJumpEnabled and infJumpMode == "hold" then
                env.task.delay(0.18, function()
                    if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(env.Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                        JumpState.holdActive = true
                    end
                end
                )
            end
        elseif _uf.KeyCode == env.Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA and env.tostring(_uf.UserInputType):find("Gamepad") then
            JumpState.controllerActive = true
        end
    end
    ) UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(_vf)
        if _vf.UserInputType == env.Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and _vf.KeyCode == env.Enum.KeyCode.Space then
            JumpState.holdPressed = false JumpState.holdActive = false
        end
        if _vf.KeyCode == env.Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA then
            JumpState.controllerActive = false
        end
    end
    )
end
) _G.PrintedHookMobileJumpButton = function(_wf)
    if not _wf or _wf.Name ~= "JumpButton" or not _wf:IsA("GuiButton") or JumpState.hooked[_wf] then
        return
    end
    JumpState.hooked[_wf] = true _wf.InputBegan:Connect(function(_xf)
        if _xf.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.Touch or not infJumpEnabled then
            return
        end
        JumpState.mobilePressed = true env.task.delay(0.12, function()
            if JumpState.mobilePressed and infJumpEnabled and infJumpMode == "hold" then
                JumpState.mobileActive = true
            end
        end
        )
    end
    ) _wf.InputEnded:Connect(function(_yf)
        if _yf.UserInputType == env.Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            JumpState.mobilePressed = false JumpState.mobileActive = false
        end
    end
    )
end
env.task.spawn(function()
    local _zf = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if not _zf then
        return
    end
    for _Af, _Bf in env.ipairs(_zf:GetDescendants()) do
        env.pcall(_G.PrintedHookMobileJumpButton, _Bf)
    end
    _zf.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(_Cf)
        env.task.defer(_G.PrintedHookMobileJumpButton, _Cf)
    end
    )
end
)
function startUnwalk()
    local _Df = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Df then
        return
    end
    local _Ef = _Df:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Ef then
        for _Ff, _Gf in env.ipairs(_Ef:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            _Gf:Stop()
        end
    end
    local _Hf = _Df:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if _Hf then
        _Nb = _Hf:Clone() _Hf:Destroy()
    end
end
function stopUnwalk()
    local _If = LocalPlayer.Character
    if _If and _Nb then
        _Nb:Clone().Parent = _If _Nb = nil
    end
end
function runDrop()
    if dropActive then
        return
    end
    if autoBatEnabled then
        autoBatEnabled = false
        if resetAutoBatMotion then
            resetAutoBatMotion()
        end
        if autoBatSetVisual then
            autoBatSetVisual(false)
        end
        if disableAutoBat then
            env.pcall(disableAutoBat)
        end
    end
    local _Jf = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Jf or not _Jf:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        return
    end
    dropActive = true
    local _Kf = env.tick()
    local _Lf = nil
    _Lf = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local _Mf = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _Nf = _Mf and _Mf:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not _Nf then
            if _Lf then
                _Lf:Disconnect()
            end
            dropActive = false
            return
        end
        if env.tick() - _Kf >= 0.2 then
            if _Lf then
                _Lf:Disconnect()
            end
            local _Of = RaycastParams.new() _Of.FilterDescendantsInstances = {_Mf} _Of.FilterType = env.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local _Pf = env.workspace:Raycast(_Nf.Position, env.Vector3.new(0, - 2000, 0), _Of)
            if _Pf then
                local _Qf = _Mf:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") _Nf.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_Nf.Position.X, _Pf.Position.Y +(_Qf and _Qf.HipHeight or(0x1 + 1)) + _Nf.Size.Y / 2,
                _Nf.Position.Z) _Nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            dropActive = false
            return
        end
        _Nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 150, _Nf.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
    end
    )
end
_wb = 0
function doAutoTPDown(_Rf)
    local _Sf = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Sf then
        return
    end
    local _Tf = _Sf:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Tf then
        return
    end
    local _Uf = _Sf:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not _Uf then
        return
    end
    if not _Rf then
        if _Uf.FloorMaterial ~= env.Enum.Material.Air then
            return
        end
        if _Tf.Position.Y < autoTPHeight then
            return
        end
    end
    local _Vf, _Wf = _Tf.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ() _Tf.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_Tf.Position.X, - 7, _Tf.Position.Z) * env.CFrame.Angles(0,
    _Wf, 0) _Tf.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
end
function startAutoTP()
    if autoTPConn then
        env.task.cancel(autoTPConn)
        autoTPConn = nil
    end
    autoTPConn = env.task.spawn(function()
        while autoTPEnabled do
            env.task.wait(0.1) env.pcall(function()
                doAutoTPDown(false)
            end
            )
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopAutoTP(_Xf)
    if autoTPConn then
        env.task.cancel(autoTPConn)
        autoTPConn = nil
    end
    if not _Xf then
        autoTPEnabled = false
    end
end
function runTPFloor()
    if suspendBodyLock then
        env.pcall(suspendBodyLock)
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        doAutoTPDown(true)
    end
    ) env.task.delay(0.6, function()
        if not autoBatEnabled and not tpBatEnabled then
            if restoreBodyLock then
                env.pcall(restoreBodyLock)
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
if not _xb then
    _xb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local _Yf = mirrorTPDownEnabled and(autoBatEnabled == true or tpBatEnabled == true)
        if not _Yf then
            if env.next(_tb) then
                env.table.clear(_tb)
            end
            return
        end
        for _Zf, _ag in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if _ag ~= LocalPlayer and _ag.Character then
                local _bg = _ag.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if _bg then
                    local _cg = _bg.Position.Y
                    local _dg = _tb[_ag.UserId]
                    if _dg and _dg - _cg >= 3 then
                        env.pcall(function()
                            local _eg = LocalPlayer.Character
                            local _fg = _eg and _eg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") _eg = _eg and _eg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if _fg and _eg and _eg.Health > 0 then
                                local _gg, _hg = _fg.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ() _fg.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_fg.Position.X, - 7, _fg.Position.Z) * env.CFrame.Angles(0,
                                _hg, 0) _fg.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
                            end
                        end
                        ) env.table.clear(_tb)
                        return
                    end
                    _tb[_ag.UserId] = _cg
                end
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
local function _ig(_jg)
    local _kg = _jg + env.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
    local _lg = RaycastParams.new() _lg.FilterType = env.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    local _mg = LocalPlayer.Character
    if _mg then
        _lg.FilterDescendantsInstances = {_mg}
    end
    _lg.IgnoreWater = true
    return env.workspace:Raycast(_kg, env.Vector3.new(0, - 80, 0), _lg)
end
local function _ng(_og, _pg)
    if not antiVoidSafeCFrame then
        return
    end
    _og.CFrame = antiVoidSafeCFrame + env.Vector3.new(0, 5, 0) _og.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) _og.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _pg.PlatformStand = false _pg.Sit = false env.pcall(function()
        _pg:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(_og, "NetworkIsSleeping", false)
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopAntiVoid()
    if antiVoidConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            antiVoidConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        antiVoidConn = nil
    end
    if antiVoidSteppedConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            antiVoidSteppedConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        antiVoidSteppedConn = nil
    end
    antiVoidEnabled = false
end
function startAntiVoid()
    stopAntiVoid()
    antiVoidEnabled = true
    antiVoidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not antiVoidEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _qg = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _rg = _qg and _qg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") _qg = _qg and _qg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not _rg or not _qg or _rg.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local _sg =(env.workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight or - 500) + 30
        local _tg = _qg.Position.Y
        local _ug = _qg.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if _rg.FloorMaterial ~= env.Enum.Material.Air and _tg > _sg + 15 then
            antiVoidSafeCFrame = _qg.CFrame antiVoidLastGround = env.tick()
        else
            local _vg = _ig(_qg)
            if _vg and _tg > _sg + 12 then
                local _wg = _qg.CFrame - _qg.CFrame.Position antiVoidSafeCFrame = env.CFrame.new(_vg.Position + env.Vector3.new(0, 3,
                0)) * _wg antiVoidLastGround = env.tick()
            end
        end
        if(_tg <= _sg or(_tg <= _sg + 8 and _ug.Y < - 90) or _tg < _sg - 20) and antiVoidSafeCFrame then
            _ng(_qg, _rg)
        end
    end
    )
    antiVoidSteppedConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not antiVoidEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _xg = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _yg = _xg and _xg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") _xg = _xg and _xg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _yg or not _xg then
            return
        end
        if _yg.Position.Y <=(env.workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight or - 500) + 30 and antiVoidSafeCFrame then
            _ng(_yg, _xg)
        end
    end
    )
end
function setAntiVoid(_zg)
    if _zg then
        startAntiVoid()
    else
        stopAntiVoid()
    end
    if setAntiVoidVisual then
        env.pcall(setAntiVoidVisual, antiVoidEnabled)
    end
end
local function _Ag()
    local _Bg = LocalPlayer.Character _Bg = _Bg and _Bg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Bg then
        return nil
    end
    local _Cg, _Dg = nil, env.math.huge
    local _Eg = env.math.clamp(env.tonumber(bodyLockRadius) or(2 * 6), 9, 20)
    for _Fg, _Gg in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if _Gg ~= LocalPlayer and _Gg.Character then
            local _Hg = _Gg.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local _Ig = _Gg.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if _Hg and _Ig and _Ig.Health > 0 then
                local _Jg =(_Hg.Position - _Bg.Position).Magnitude
                if _Jg <= _Eg and _Jg < _Dg then
                    _Dg = _Jg _Cg = _Gg
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return _Cg
end
function startBodyLock()
    if bodyLockConn then
        return
    end
    bodyLockEnabled = true
    bodyLockConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not bodyLockEnabled then
            return
        end
        if autoBatEnabled or tpBatEnabled then
            local _Kg = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if _Kg then
                _Kg.AutoRotate = true
            end
            return
        end
        local _Lg = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _Mg = _Lg and _Lg:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _Ng = _Lg and _Lg:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _Mg or not _Ng or _Ng.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local _Og = _Ag()
        if _Og and _Og.Character and _Og.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local _Pg = _Og.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
            local _Qg = _Mg.Position
            local _Rg = env.Vector3.new(_Pg.X, _Qg.Y, _Pg.Z)
            if(_Rg - _Qg).Magnitude > 0.1 then
                _Ng.AutoRotate = false _Mg.CFrame = _Mg.CFrame:Lerp(env.CFrame.lookAt(_Qg, _Rg), 0.22) _Mg.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _Mg.AssemblyAngularVelocity * env.Vector3.new(0.5,
                0.25, 0.5)
            end
        else
            _Ng.AutoRotate = true
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopBodyLock()
    bodyLockEnabled = false
    if bodyLockConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            bodyLockConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        bodyLockConn = nil
    end
    local _Sg = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Sg then
        _Sg.AutoRotate = true
    end
end
_printedBodyLockSuspended = false
function forceBodyLockOffForAimbot()
    if bodyLockEnabled or _G.PrintedBodyLockEnabled then
        _printedBodyLockSuspended = true
        bodyLockEnabled = false _G.PrintedBodyLockEnabled = false env.pcall(stopBodyLock)
        if setBodyLockVisual then
            env.pcall(setBodyLockVisual, false)
        end
    end
end
function suspendBodyLock()
    forceBodyLockOffForAimbot()
end
function restoreBodyLock()
    if not _printedBodyLockSuspended then
        return
    end
    _printedBodyLockSuspended = false
    bodyLockEnabled = true _G.PrintedBodyLockEnabled = true env.pcall(startBodyLock)
    if setBodyLockVisual then
        env.pcall(setBodyLockVisual, true)
    end
end
local function _Tg()
    local _Ug = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Ug then
        return nil
    end
    for _Vg, _Wg in env.ipairs(_Ug:GetChildren()) do
        if _Wg:IsA("Tool") then
            local _Xg = _Wg.Name:lower()
            if _Xg:find("medusa") or _Xg:find("head") or _Xg:find("stone") then
                return _Wg
            end
        end
    end
    local _Yg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if _Yg then
        for _Zg, _ah in env.ipairs(_Yg:GetChildren()) do
            if _ah:IsA("Tool") then
                local _bh = _ah.Name:lower()
                if _bh:find("medusa") or _bh:find("head") or _bh:find("stone") then
                    return _ah
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function useMedusaCounter()
    if medusaDebounce then
        return
    end
    if env.tick() - medusaLastUsed < 25 then
        return
    end
    local _ch = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _ch then
        return
    end
    medusaDebounce = true
    local _dh = _Tg()
    if not _dh then
        medusaDebounce = false
        return
    end
    if _dh.Parent ~= _ch then
        local _eh = _ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if _eh then
            _eh:EquipTool(_dh)
        end
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _dh:Activate()
    end
    )
    medusaLastUsed = env.tick()
    medusaDebounce = false
end
function onMedusaStoned()
    local _fh = env.tick()
    if _fh <(_medusaStoneUntil or(0x8 - 8)) - 2.5 then
        return
    end
    _medusaStoneUntil = _fh + 3
    _medusaGrabResumeAt = _fh + 1.7
    if flag18 then
        flag18 = false
        if _sb.progress then
            env.pcall(function()
                _sb.progress:Disconnect()
            end
            ) _sb.progress = nil
        end
        if _G.resetProgressBar then
            _G.resetProgressBar()
        end
        if textLabel2 then
            textLabel2.Text = "75%"
        end
        if frame then
            frame.Size = env.UDim2.new(0.75, 0, 1, 0)
        end
    end
    if tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
        _medusaStealWasOn = true
        if stopNormalSteal then
            env.pcall(stopNormalSteal)
        end
    end
    env.task.delay(1.7, function()
        if env.tick() <(_medusaGrabResumeAt or(0x13 - 0x13)) - 0.05 then
            return
        end
        if _medusaStealWasOn or tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
            _medusaStealWasOn = false tbl19.AutoStealEnabled = true
            if startAutoSteal then
                env.pcall(startAutoSteal)
            end
            if textLabel2 then
                textLabel2.Text = "0%"
            end
            if frame then
                frame.Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
function isMedusaStoneLocked()
    return env.tick() <(_medusaGrabResumeAt or(0x14 - 20))
end
function setupMedusa(_gh)
    for _hh, _ih in env.pairs(_sb.anchor) do
        env.pcall(function()
            _ih:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    _sb.anchor = {}
    if not _gh then
        return
    end
    local function _jh(_kh)
        if not _kh:IsA("BasePart") then
            return
        end
        local function _lh()
            if _kh and _kh.Parent and _kh.Anchored and _kh.Transparency >= 0.9 then
                env.pcall(onMedusaStoned)
                if medusaCounterEnabled then
                    useMedusaCounter()
                end
            end
        end
        env.table.insert(_sb.anchor, _kh:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(_lh)) env.table.insert(_sb.anchor,
        _kh:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(_lh))
    end
    for _mh, _nh in env.ipairs(_gh:GetDescendants()) do
        _jh(_nh)
    end
    env.table.insert(_sb.anchor, _gh.DescendantAdded:Connect(_jh))
end
function stopMedusaCounter()
    for _oh, _ph in env.pairs(_sb.anchor) do
        env.pcall(function()
            _ph:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    _sb.anchor = {}
    if _sb.medusaScan then
        env.pcall(function()
            _sb.medusaScan:Disconnect()
        end
        ) _sb.medusaScan = nil
    end
end
local _qh = {"Bat", "Slap", "Iron Slap", "Gold Slap", "Diamond Slap", "Emerald Slap", "Dark Matter Slap", "Flame Slap", "Nuclear Slap",
"Galaxy Slap", "Glitched Slap",}
local function _rh()
    local _sh = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _sh then
        return nil
    end
    local _th = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    for _uh, _vh in env.ipairs(_qh) do
        local _wh = _sh:FindFirstChild(_vh) or(_th and _th:FindFirstChild(_vh))
        if _wh and _wh:IsA("Tool") then
            return _wh
        end
    end
    for _xh, _yh in env.ipairs(_sh:GetChildren()) do
        if _yh:IsA("Tool") then
            local _zh = _yh.Name:lower()
            if _zh:find("bat") or _zh:find("slap") then
                return _yh
            end
        end
    end
    if _th then
        for _Ah, _Bh in env.ipairs(_th:GetChildren()) do
            if _Bh:IsA("Tool") then
                local _Ch = _Bh.Name:lower()
                if _Ch:find("bat") or _Ch:find("slap") then
                    return _Bh
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function _Dh(_Eh, _Fh)
    if not _Eh or not _Fh then
        return
    end
    local _Gh = _Fh:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Eh.Parent ~= _Fh and _Gh then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Gh:EquipTool(_Eh)
        end
        )
    end
    local _Hh = _Eh:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or _Eh:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
    if _Hh then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Hh:FireServer()
        end
        ) env.pcall(function()
            _Hh:FireServer()
        end
        )
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _Eh:Activate()
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        _Eh:Activate()
    end
    )
end
local function _Ih()
    if not batCounterEnabled then
        return
    end
    local _Jh = env.tick()
    if _Jh - _Gb < 0.08 then
        return
    end
    local _Kh = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Kh then
        return
    end
    local _Lh = _Kh:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not _Lh then
        return
    end
    local _Mh = _Lh:GetState()
    if _Mh == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or _Mh == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or _Mh == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or _Lh.PlatformStand then
        _Gb = _Jh
        local _Nh = _rh()
        if _Nh then
            _Dh(_Nh, _Kh)
        end
    end
end
function startBatCounter()
    if _sb.batCounter then
        return
    end
    _sb.batCounter = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(_Ih)
    local function _Oh(_Ph)
        _Ph = _Ph and _Ph:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _Ph then
            return
        end
        local _Qh = _Ph.StateChanged:Connect(function(_xh, new)
            if not batCounterEnabled then
                return
            end
            if new == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or new == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or new == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                _Ih()
            end
        end
        ) _sb.batStateConns = _sb.batStateConns or {} env.table.insert(_sb.batStateConns, _Qh)
    end
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        _Oh(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
    if not _sb.batCharAdded then
        _sb.batCharAdded = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(_Rh)
            env.task.defer(_Oh, _Rh)
        end
        )
    end
end
function stopBatCounter()
    if _sb.batCounter then
        _sb.batCounter:Disconnect() _sb.batCounter = nil
    end
    if _sb.batStateConns then
        for _Sh, _Th in env.ipairs(_sb.batStateConns) do
            env.pcall(function()
                _Th:Disconnect()
            end
            )
        end
        _sb.batStateConns = {}
    end
    if _sb.batCharAdded then
        env.pcall(function()
            _sb.batCharAdded:Disconnect()
        end
        ) _sb.batCharAdded = nil
    end
    _Gb = 0
end
local function _Uh()
    if laggerToggled then
        return env.tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or(3341 - 0xcb3)
    end
    return env.tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or(194 % 156 + 0x14)
end
local function _Vh()
    local _Wh = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Wh then
        return nil
    end
    for _Xh, _Yh in env.ipairs(_qh) do
        local _Zh = _Wh:FindFirstChild(_Yh)
        if _Zh and _Zh:IsA("Tool") then
            return _Zh
        end
    end
    local _ai = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if _ai then
        for _bi, _ci in env.ipairs(_qh) do
            local _di = _ai:FindFirstChild(_ci)
            if _di and _di:IsA("Tool") then
                local _ei = _Wh:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _ei then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _ei:EquipTool(_di)
                    end
                    )
                end
                return _di
            end
        end
    end
    for _fi, _gi in env.ipairs(_Wh:GetChildren()) do
        if _gi:IsA("Tool") and(_gi.Name:lower():find("bat") or _gi.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
            return _gi
        end
    end
    if _ai then
        for _hi, _ii in env.ipairs(_ai:GetChildren()) do
            if _ii:IsA("Tool") and(_ii.Name:lower():find("bat") or _ii.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
                local _ji = _Wh:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _ji then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _ji:EquipTool(_ii)
                    end
                    )
                end
                return _ii
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function _ki()
    if _Jb or not autoSwingEnabled then
        return
    end
    _Jb = true env.pcall(function()
        local _li = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _li then
            return
        end
        local _mi = _Vh()
        if _mi then
            if _mi.Parent ~= _li then
                local _ni = _li:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if _ni then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _ni:EquipTool(_mi)
                    end
                    )
                end
            end
            env.pcall(function()
                _mi:Activate()
            end
            )
        end
    end
    ) env.task.delay(0.25, function()
        _Jb = false
    end
    )
end
local function _oi()
    local _pi = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _pi then
        return nil, env.math.huge
    end
    local _qi, _ri = nil, env.math.huge
    for _si, _ti in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if _ti ~= LocalPlayer and _ti.Character then
            local _ui = _ti.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local _vi = _ti.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if _ui and _vi and _vi.Health > 0 then
                local _wi =(_ui.Position - _pi.Position).Magnitude
                if _wi < _ri then
                    _ri = _wi _qi = _ti
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return _qi, _ri
end
function resetAutoBatMotion()
    local _xi = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _yi = _xi and _xi:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local _zi = _xi and _xi:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _yi then
        _yi.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(0, _yi.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.3, 0) _yi.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
    end
    if _zi then
        _zi.AutoRotate = _Ib == nil and(1 == 1) or _Ib _zi.PlatformStand = false env.pcall(function()
            _zi:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
        )
    end
    _Ib = nil
end
function stopEnvoyOrV1Aimbot()
    if _Hb then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Hb:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        _Hb = nil
    end
    _Lb = nil
    local _Ai = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _Bi = _Ai and _Ai:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if _Bi then
        _Bi.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _Bi.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
    end
    _Ai = _Ai and _Ai:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Ai then
        _Ai.AutoRotate = _Ib == nil and(2 > 1) or _Ib
    end
    _Ib = nil
end
function startEnvoyOrV1Aimbot()
    stopEnvoyOrV1Aimbot() forceBodyLockOffForAimbot()
    aimbotMode = "V1"
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        env.pcall(stopAutoLeft)
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        env.pcall(stopAutoRight)
    end
    local _Ci = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Ci then
        if _Ib == nil then
            _Ib = _Ci.AutoRotate
        end
        _Ci.AutoRotate = false
    end
    _Hb = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not autoBatEnabled or aimbotMode ~= "V1" then
            return
        end
        local _Di = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _Di then
            return
        end
        local _Ei = _Di:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _Fi = _Di:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _Ei or not _Fi then
            return
        end
        if not _Di:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local _Gi = _Vh()
            if _Gi then
                env.pcall(function()
                    _Fi:EquipTool(_Gi)
                end
                )
            end
        end
        local _Hi = _oi()
        if not _Hi or not _Hi.Character then
            return
        end
        local _Ii = _Hi.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not _Ii then
            return
        end
        _Lb = _Ii
        local _Ji = _Ii.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local _Ki = _Ei.Position
        local _Li = _Ii.Position
        local _Mi = _Li + _Ji * 0.14 + _Ii.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3 - _Ki
        local _Ni = env.Vector3.new(_Mi.X, 0, _Mi.Z).Unit
        local _Oi = _Uh()
        local _Pi =(_Li.Y + 3.7 - _Ki.Y) * 19.5 + _Ji.Y * 0.8
        local _Qi = _Fi.FloorMaterial == env.Enum.Material.Air and _Pi or env.math.max(_Pi, 13) _Ei.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _Ei.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(env.Vector3.new(_Ni.X * _Oi,
        env.math.clamp(_Qi, - 70, 110), _Ni.Z * _Oi), 0.8)
        local _Ri = _Li + _Ji * env.math.clamp(_Ji.Magnitude / 120, 0.05, 0.2)
        if(_Ri - _Ki).Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local _Si = env.CFrame.lookAt(_Ki, _Ri)
            local _Ti, _Ui, _Vi =(_Ei.CFrame:Inverse() * _Si):ToEulerAnglesXYZ() _Ei.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _Ei.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(env.Vector3.new(env.math.clamp(_Ti,
            - 2.5, 2.5) * 42, env.math.clamp(_Ui, - 2.5, 2.5) * 42, env.math.clamp(_Vi, - 2.5, 2.5) * 42))
        end
        if autoSwingEnabled then
            _ki()
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopAntiBypassAimbotConn()
    if _Hb then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Hb:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        _Hb = nil
    end
    _Jb = false
    local _Wi = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _Xi = _Wi and _Wi:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if _Xi then
        _Xi.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _Xi.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
    end
    local _Yi = _Wi and _Wi:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Yi then
        _Yi.AutoRotate = _Ib == nil and(2 > 1) or _Ib _Yi.PlatformStand = false env.pcall(function()
            _Yi:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
        )
    end
    _Ib = nil
end
function startAntiBypassAimbotConn()
    stopAntiBypassAimbotConn() forceBodyLockOffForAimbot()
    if aimbotMode ~= "AntiBypass" then
        aimbotMode = "V2"
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        env.pcall(stopAutoLeft)
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        env.pcall(stopAutoRight)
    end
    local _Zi = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Zi then
        if _Ib == nil then
            _Ib = _Zi.AutoRotate
        end
        _Zi.AutoRotate = false
    end
    _Jb = false
    _Hb = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not autoBatEnabled then
            return
        end
        if aimbotMode ~= "V2" and aimbotMode ~= "AntiBypass" then
            return
        end
        local _aj = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _aj then
            return
        end
        local _bj = _aj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _cj = _aj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _bj or not _cj then
            return
        end
        if not _aj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local _dj = _Vh()
            if _dj then
                env.pcall(function()
                    _cj:EquipTool(_dj)
                end
                )
            end
        end
        local _ej, _fj = _oi()
        if not _ej or not _ej.Character then
            return
        end
        local _gj = _ej.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not _gj then
            return
        end
        local _hj = _bj.Position
        local _ij = _gj.Position
        local _jj = _ij - _hj
        local _kj = env.Vector3.new(_jj.X, 0, _jj.Z)
        local _lj = _kj.Magnitude > 0 and _kj.Unit or env.Vector3.zero
        local _mj = _Uh()
        local _nj =(_ij.Y + 3.7 - _hj.Y) * 19.5
        local _oj = _cj.FloorMaterial == env.Enum.Material.Air and _nj or env.math.max(_nj, 13) _bj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _bj.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(env.Vector3.new(_lj.X * _mj,
        env.math.clamp(_oj, - 70, 110), _lj.Z * _mj), 0.8)
        if(_ij - _hj).Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local _pj = env.CFrame.lookAt(_hj, _ij)
            local _qj, _rj, _sj =(_bj.CFrame:Inverse() * _pj):ToEulerAnglesXYZ() _bj.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _bj.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(env.Vector3.new(env.math.clamp(_qj,
            - 2.5, 2.5) * 42, env.math.clamp(_rj, - 2.5, 2.5) * 42, env.math.clamp(_sj, - 2.5, 2.5) * 42))
        end
        if _fj <= 8 then
            _ki()
        end
    end
    )
end
function applyAimbotMode()
    local _tj = env.tostring(aimbotMode or "V1")
    if _tj == "AntiBypass" or _tj == "Anti Bypass" or _tj == "V2" or _tj == "Kawatan" then
        aimbotMode = "V2" _G.PrintedAntiBypassAimbot = false
    else
        aimbotMode = "V1" _G.PrintedAntiBypassAimbot = false
    end
    AUTO_BAT_SPEED = env.tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or(16 + 21 + 21)
    if fn44 then
        env.pcall(fn44, aimbotMode)
    end
end
function stopKawatanAimbotConn()
    stopAntiBypassAimbotConn()
end
function startKawatanAimbotConn()
    startAntiBypassAimbotConn()
end
function enableAutoBat()
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoLeft()
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    if autoTPEnabled then
        stopAutoTP()
        if setAutoTPVisual then
            setAutoTPVisual(false)
        end
    end
    autoBatEnabled = true env.pcall(function()
        local _uj = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _uj then
            return
        end
        local _vj = _uj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _vj then
            return
        end
        local _wj = _Vh()
        if _wj and _wj.Parent ~= _uj then
            env.pcall(function()
                _vj:EquipTool(_wj)
            end
            )
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(stopAntiBypassAimbotConn) env.pcall(stopEnvoyOrV1Aimbot) applyAimbotMode()
    if aimbotMode == "V1" then
        startEnvoyOrV1Aimbot()
    else
        aimbotMode = "V2" startAntiBypassAimbotConn()
    end
end
function disableAutoBat()
    autoBatEnabled = false env.pcall(stopEnvoyOrV1Aimbot) env.pcall(stopAntiBypassAimbotConn)
    local _xj = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _yj = _xj and _xj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    _xj = _xj and _xj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _yj then
        _yj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _yj.AssemblyLinearVelocity * 0.3 _yj.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
    end
    if _xj then
        _xj.AutoRotate = _Kb == nil and(2 > 1) or _Kb
    end
    _Kb = nil
    if restoreBodyLock then
        env.pcall(restoreBodyLock)
    end
end
function queueAutoBatStart()
    if _G.PrintedSafeModeTryStart and not _G.PrintedSafeModeTryStart("You clicked Aimbot / Auto Bat", "AutoBat") then
        if autoBatSetVisual then
            autoBatSetVisual(false)
        end
        return
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoLeft()
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    enableAutoBat()
end
function stopAutoLeft()
    if _Cb then
        _Cb:Disconnect()
        _Cb = nil
    end
    _Eb = 1
    local _zj = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _zj then
        _zj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false)
    end
    if autoLeftSetVisual then
        autoLeftSetVisual(false)
    end
    if _updateMobileAutoLeft then
        _updateMobileAutoLeft(false)
    end
end
function stopAutoRight()
    if _Db then
        _Db:Disconnect()
        _Db = nil
    end
    _Fb = 1
    local _Aj = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Aj then
        _Aj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false)
    end
    if autoRightSetVisual then
        autoRightSetVisual(false)
    end
    if _updateMobileAutoRight then
        _updateMobileAutoRight(false)
    end
end
function startAutoLeft()
    if _Cb then
        _Cb:Disconnect()
    end
    _Eb = 1
    _Cb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoLeftEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _Bj = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _Bj then
            return
        end
        local _Cj = _Bj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _Dj = _Bj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _Cj or not _Dj then
            return
        end
        if _Zb(_Dj) then
            _Dj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false)
            return
        end
        local _Ej = _Yb()
        if _Eb == 1 then
            local _Fj = _Cj.Position
            if(env.Vector3.new(_yb.X, _Cj.Position.Y, _yb.Z) - _Fj).Magnitude < 1 then
                _Eb = 2
                local _Gj = _zb - _Cj.Position
                local _Hj = env.Vector3.new(_Gj.X, 0, _Gj.Z).Unit _Dj:Move(_Hj, false) _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Hj.X * _Ej,
                _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Hj.Z * _Ej)
                return
            end
            local _Ij = _yb - _Cj.Position
            local _Jj = env.Vector3.new(_Ij.X, 0, _Ij.Z).Unit _Dj:Move(_Jj, false) _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Jj.X * _Ej,
            _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Jj.Z * _Ej)
        elseif _Eb == 2 then
            local _Kj = _Cj.Position
            if(env.Vector3.new(_zb.X, _Cj.Position.Y, _zb.Z) - _Kj).Magnitude < 1 then
                _Dj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false) _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero autoLeftEnabled = false
                if _Cb then
                    _Cb:Disconnect() _Cb = nil
                end
                _Eb = 1
                if autoLeftSetVisual then
                    autoLeftSetVisual(false)
                end
                if _updateMobileAutoLeft then
                    _updateMobileAutoLeft(false)
                end
                return
            end
            local _Lj = _zb - _Cj.Position
            local _Mj = env.Vector3.new(_Lj.X, 0, _Lj.Z).Unit _Dj:Move(_Mj, false) _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Mj.X * _Ej,
            _Cj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Mj.Z * _Ej)
        end
    end
    )
end
function startAutoRight()
    if _Db then
        _Db:Disconnect()
    end
    _Fb = 1
    _Db = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoRightEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _Nj = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _Nj then
            return
        end
        local _Oj = _Nj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _Pj = _Nj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _Oj or not _Pj then
            return
        end
        if _Zb(_Pj) then
            _Pj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false)
            return
        end
        local _Qj = _Yb()
        if _Fb == 1 then
            local _Rj = _Oj.Position
            if(env.Vector3.new(_Ab.X, _Oj.Position.Y, _Ab.Z) - _Rj).Magnitude < 1 then
                _Fb = 2
                local _Sj = _Bb - _Oj.Position
                local _Tj = env.Vector3.new(_Sj.X, 0, _Sj.Z).Unit _Pj:Move(_Tj, false) _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Tj.X * _Qj,
                _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Tj.Z * _Qj)
                return
            end
            local _Uj = _Ab - _Oj.Position
            local _Vj = env.Vector3.new(_Uj.X, 0, _Uj.Z).Unit _Pj:Move(_Vj, false) _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Vj.X * _Qj,
            _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Vj.Z * _Qj)
        elseif _Fb == 2 then
            local _Wj = _Oj.Position
            if(env.Vector3.new(_Bb.X, _Oj.Position.Y, _Bb.Z) - _Wj).Magnitude < 1 then
                _Pj:Move(env.Vector3.zero, false) _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero autoRightEnabled = false
                if _Db then
                    _Db:Disconnect() _Db = nil
                end
                _Fb = 1
                if autoRightSetVisual then
                    autoRightSetVisual(false)
                end
                if _updateMobileAutoRight then
                    _updateMobileAutoRight(false)
                end
                return
            end
            local _Xj = _Bb - _Oj.Position
            local _Yj = env.Vector3.new(_Xj.X, 0, _Xj.Z).Unit _Pj:Move(_Yj, false) _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Yj.X * _Qj,
            _Oj.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, _Yj.Z * _Qj)
        end
    end
    )
end
function queueAutoLeftStart()
    if _G.PrintedSafeModeTryStart and not _G.PrintedSafeModeTryStart("You clicked Auto Left", "AutoLeft") then
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        return
    end
    autoLeftEnabled = true
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoRight()
    end
    if autoBatEnabled then
        disableAutoBat()
        if autoBatSetVisual then
            autoBatSetVisual(false)
        end
    end
    startAutoLeft()
end

        end
        return
    end
    autoRightEnabled = true
    if autoLeftEnabled then

        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        stopAutoLeft()

    if autoBatEnabled then
