        if autoBatSetVisual then
            autoBatSetVisual(false)
        end
    end
    startAutoRight()
end
local function _Zj()
    return env.tick() <(_medusaGrabResumeAt or(8 - 0x8))
end
function isMyPlotByName(_ak)
    local _bk = env.workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not _bk then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    local _ck = _bk:FindFirstChild(_ak)
    if not _ck then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    local _dk = _ck:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if _dk then
        local _ek = _dk:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if _ek and _ek:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            return _ek.Enabled == true
        end
    end
    return(2 < 1)
end
function checkMyPlot()
    for _fk, _gk in env.pairs({left = env.Vector3.new(- 476.75, 10.46, 7.11), right = env.Vector3.new(- 476.75, 10.46,
    114.11)}) do
        for _hk, _ik in env.ipairs(env.workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if _ik:IsA("BasePart") and _ik.Name == "PlotSign" then
                if(_ik.Position - _gk).Magnitude < 20 then
                    local _jk = _ik.Parent or _ik
                    for _kk, _lk in env.ipairs(_jk:GetDescendants()) do
                        if _lk:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                            for _mk, _nk in env.ipairs(_lk:GetDescendants()) do
                                if _nk:IsA("TextLabel") and _nk.Text ~= "" then
                                    if _nk.Text:find(LocalPlayer.Name) or(LocalPlayer.DisplayName and _nk.Text:find(LocalPlayer.DisplayName)) then
                                        return _fk
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function _ok()
    local _pk = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _pk then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local _qk = _pk:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _qk then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local _rk = env.workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not _rk then
        return nil, nil
    end
    local _sk, _tk, _uk = nil, env.math.huge, nil
    local _vk = env.tonumber(tbl19 and tbl19.StealRadius) or ENVY_STEAL_RADIUS or(37 + 23)
    local function _wk(_xk)
        if not _xk or not _xk:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
            return(2 < 1)
        end
        local _yk = env.string.lower(env.tostring(_xk.ActionText or ""))
        local _zk = env.string.lower(env.tostring(_xk.ObjectText or ""))
        if _yk:find("steal", 1, true) or _zk:find("steal", 1, true) then
            return(2 > 1)
        end
        return _xk.Enabled ~= false and _yk ~= ""
    end
    for _Ak, _Bk in env.ipairs(_rk:GetChildren()) do
        if not isMyPlotByName(_Bk.Name) then
            local _Ck = _Bk:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums") or _Bk:FindFirstChild("Podiums")
            if _Ck then
                for _Dk, _Ek in env.ipairs(_Ck:GetChildren()) do
                    for _Fk, _Gk in env.ipairs(_Ek:GetDescendants()) do
                        if _Gk:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and _wk(_Gk) then
                            local _Hk = _Gk.Parent
                            if _Hk and _Hk:IsA("Attachment") then
                                _Hk = _Hk.Parent
                            end
                            local _Ik = nil
                            if _Hk and _Hk:IsA("BasePart") then
                                _Ik = _Hk.Position
                            elseif _Gk.Parent and _Gk.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                                _Ik = _Gk.Parent.Position
                            elseif _Gk.Parent and _Gk.Parent:IsA("Attachment") and _Gk.Parent.Parent and _Gk.Parent.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
                                _Ik = _Gk.Parent.Parent.Position
                            end
                            if _Ik then
                                local _Jk =(_Ik - _qk.Position).Magnitude
                                if _Jk <= _vk and _Jk < _tk then
                                    _tk = _Jk _sk = _Gk _uk = _Ek.Name
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return _sk, _uk
end
local function _Kk(_Lk)
    env.pcall(function()
        if _Lk.InputHoldBegin then
            _Lk:InputHoldBegin()
        end
    end
    )
end
local function _Mk(_Nk)
    env.pcall(function()
        if _Nk.InputHoldEnd then
            _Nk:InputHoldEnd()
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        if fireproximityprompt then
            fireproximityprompt(_Nk)
        end
    end
    )
end
local function _Ok()
    local _Pk = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Pk and _Zb(_Pk) then
        _vb = env.tick() + 2
        return(1 == 1)
    end
    if env.tick() <(_vb or(3 - 3)) then
        return(1 == 1)
    end
    return(1 ~= 1)
end
function executeSteal(_Qk, _Rk)
    if flag18 then
        return
    end
    if _Ok() then
        return
    end
    if not _Qk or not _Qk.Parent then
        return
    end
    if _Zj() then
        return
    end
    if not tbl19.Data[_Qk] then
        tbl19.Data[_Qk] = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true} env.pcall(function()
            if getconnections then
                for _Sk, _Tk in env.ipairs(getconnections(_Qk.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                    if _Tk.Function then
                        env.table.insert(tbl19.Data[_Qk].hold, _Tk.Function)
                    end
                end
                for _Uk, _Vk in env.ipairs(getconnections(_Qk.Triggered)) do
                    if _Vk.Function then
                        env.table.insert(tbl19.Data[_Qk].trigger, _Vk.Function)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        )
    end
    local _Wk = tbl19.Data[_Qk]
    if not _Wk.ready then
        return
    end
    _Wk.ready = false
    flag18 = true
    _ub = env.tick()
    if not(_G.__PrintedStealGui and _G.__PrintedStealGui.Parent) then
        env.pcall(function()
            if type(_G.PrintedRebuildStealBar) == "function" then
                _G.PrintedRebuildStealBar()
            elseif _G._PrintedBG and _G._PrintedBG.gui then
                buildStealBar(_G._PrintedBG.gui)
            end
        end
        )
    end
    if _G.setStealProgress then
        _G.setStealProgress(0.02)
    else
        env.warn("[Printed] setStealProgress missing \226\128\148 steal bar not built")
    end
    env.task.spawn(function()
        for _Xk, _Yk in env.ipairs(_Wk.hold) do
            env.task.spawn(_Yk)
        end
        if # _Wk.hold == 0 then
            _Kk(_Qk)
        end
        local _Zk = env.tick() autoGrabStopTime = 0.96 autoGrabSetDelayRadius = 9 tbl19.StealDuration = 1.3
        local function _al()
            _Wk.ready = true flag18 = false
            if _G.resetProgressBar then
                env.pcall(_G.resetProgressBar)
            end
            if frame then
                env.pcall(function()
                    frame.Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                end
                )
            end
            if textLabel2 then
                env.pcall(function()
                    textLabel2.Text = "0%"
                end
                )
            end
        end
        if autoGrabStopEnabled then
            while(1 == 1) do
                if flag18 and tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
                    local _bl = env.tick() - _Zk
                    if not(autoGrabStopTime <= _bl) then
                        if _G.setStealProgress then
                            _G.setStealProgress(env.math.clamp(_bl / 1.3, 0, 1))
                        end
                        local _cl = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if _cl then
                            local _dl =(_cl.Position - _Qk.Parent.Parent.Position).Magnitude >(tbl19.StealRadius or ENVY_STEAL_RADIUS)
                            if _dl then
                                env.task.wait()
                                continue
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                break
            end
            if _G.setStealProgress then
                _G.setStealProgress(env.math.clamp(autoGrabStopTime / 1.3, 0, 1))
            end
            local _el = env.math.max(2.99 - autoGrabStopTime - env.math.max(1.3 - autoGrabStopTime, 0), 0.05)
            local _fl = env.tick()
            local _gl = nil
            while(1 == 1) do
                if flag18 and tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
                    if _el <= env.tick() - _fl then
                        _gl = 2
                        break
                    elseif not _Qk.Parent or not _Qk.Parent.Parent then
                        _gl = 3
                        break
                    else
                        local _hl = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if _hl then
                            local _il =(_hl.Position - _Qk.Parent.Parent.Position).Magnitude
                            if _il <= autoGrabSetDelayRadius then
                                _gl = 1
                                break
                            elseif not((tbl19.StealRadius or ENVY_STEAL_RADIUS) < _il) then
                                env.task.wait()
                                continue
                            end
                        else
                            env.task.wait()
                            continue
                        end
                    end
                else
                    _gl = 1
                    break
                end
                break
            end
            if _gl ~= 1 then
                if _gl == 2 then
                    _al() env.task.wait()
                    local _jl, _kl = _ok()
                    if _jl then
                        executeSteal(_jl, _kl)
                    end
                    return
                end
                if _gl == 3 then
                    _al()
                    return
                end
                _al()
                return
            end
            if flag18 and tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
                local _ll = env.tick()
                local _ml = env.math.max(1.3 - autoGrabStopTime, 0.05)
                local _nl = env.math.clamp(autoGrabStopTime / 1.3, 0, 1)
                while(1 == 1) do
                    local _ol = env.math.clamp((env.tick() - _ll) / _ml, 0, 1)
                    if _G.setStealProgress then
                        _G.setStealProgress(_nl + _ol *(1 - _nl))
                    end
                    if not(_ol >= 1 and(1 == 1)) then
                        env.task.wait()
                        continue
                    end
                    break
                end
                env.pcall(function()
                    for _pl, _ql in env.ipairs(_Wk.trigger) do
                        env.task.spawn(_ql)
                    end
                    local _rl = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("StealAnimal")
                    if _rl and _Rk then
                        _rl:FireServer(_Rk)
                    end
                    if _Qk then
                        env.pcall(function()
                            _Qk:Fire()
                        end
                        ) _Mk(_Qk)
                    end
                end
                )
            end
        else
            while(2 > 1) do
                if flag18 and tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
                    local _sl = env.tick() - _Zk
                    local _tl = env.math.clamp(_sl / 1.3, 0, 1)
                    if _G.setStealProgress then
                        _G.setStealProgress(_tl)
                    end
                    local _ul = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local _vl = false
                    if _ul then
                        _vl =(_ul.Position - _Qk.Parent.Parent.Position).Magnitude >(tbl19.StealRadius or ENVY_STEAL_RADIUS)
                    end
                    if not _vl then
                        if _sl >= 1.3 then
                            env.pcall(function()
                                for _wl, _xl in env.ipairs(_Wk.trigger) do
                                    env.task.spawn(_xl)
                                end
                                local _yl = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("StealAnimal")
                                if _yl and _Rk then
                                    _yl:FireServer(_Rk)
                                end
                                if _Qk then
                                    env.pcall(function()
                                        _Qk:Fire()
                                    end
                                    ) _Mk(_Qk)
                                end
                            end
                            )
                            break
                        else
                            env.task.wait()
                            continue
                        end
                    end
                end
                break
            end
        end
        _al()
    end
    )
end
function startNormalSteal()
    if _sb.autoSteal then
        return
    end
    tbl19.StealRadius = ENVY_STEAL_RADIUS tbl19.StealDuration = ENVY_STEAL_DURATION _sb.autoSteal = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
            if flag18 == false and _G.resetProgressBar then
                env.pcall(_G.resetProgressBar)
            end
            return
        end
        if flag18 then
            return
        end
        if _Ok() then
            return
        end
        local _zl, _Al = _ok()
        if _zl then
            env.pcall(executeSteal, _zl, _Al)
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopNormalSteal()
    if _sb.autoSteal then
        _sb.autoSteal:Disconnect() _sb.autoSteal = nil
    end
    if _sb.progress then
        _sb.progress:Disconnect() _sb.progress = nil
    end
    flag18 = false
    if _G.resetProgressBar then
        _G.resetProgressBar()
    end
end
function startAutoSteal()
    tbl19.AutoStealEnabled = true
    if env.tostring(str7) == "Timing" then
        autoGrabStopEnabled = false
    else
        autoGrabStopEnabled = true
    end
    stopNormalSteal() startNormalSteal()
    if not(_G.__PrintedStealGui and _G.__PrintedStealGui.Parent) then
        env.pcall(function()
            if _G._PrintedBG and _G._PrintedBG.gui then
                buildStealBar(_G._PrintedBG.gui)
            end
        end
        )
    end
end
function stopAutoSteal()
    tbl19.AutoStealEnabled = false stopNormalSteal()
    if _G.resetProgressBar then
        _G.resetProgressBar()
    end
end
function applyStealMode()
    local _Bl = env.tostring(str7 or "Semi")
    if _Bl == "Timing" or _Bl == "Envy" or _Bl == "Timed" then
        str7 = "Timing" _G.PrintedStealMode = "Timing" _G.PrintedSemiStealActive = false _G.PrintedNormalSteal = false _G.PrintedTimingSteal = true tbl19.StealDuration = env.tonumber(tbl19.StealDuration) or 1.4 tbl19.StealRadius = env.tonumber(n32) or tbl19.StealRadius or(0x2 * 30)
        if radInput then
            radInput.Text = env.tostring(tbl19.StealRadius)
        end
        if tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
            if stopAutoSteal then
                env.pcall(stopAutoSteal)
            end
            if _G.PrintedStartTimingSteal then
                env.pcall(_G.PrintedStartTimingSteal)
            end
        elseif _G.PrintedStopTimingSteal then
            env.pcall(_G.PrintedStopTimingSteal)
        end
    else
        str7 = "Semi" _G.PrintedStealMode = "Semi" _G.PrintedSemiStealActive = true _G.PrintedNormalSteal = true _G.PrintedTimingSteal = false tbl19.StealDuration = 1.3 tbl19.StealRadius = env.tonumber(n32) or tbl19.StealRadius or(31 % 12 + 53)
        if radInput then
            radInput.Text = env.tostring(tbl19.StealRadius)
        end
        if _G.PrintedStopTimingSteal then
            env.pcall(_G.PrintedStopTimingSteal)
        end
        if tbl19.AutoStealEnabled and startAutoSteal then
            env.pcall(startAutoSteal)
        else
            stopAutoSteal()
        end
    end
end
_G.PrintedStartTimingSteal = function()
    str7 = "Timing" tbl19.AutoStealEnabled = true
    autoGrabStopEnabled = false stopNormalSteal() startNormalSteal()
end
_G.PrintedStopTimingSteal = function()
    autoGrabStopEnabled = true
    if not tbl19.AutoStealEnabled then
        stopNormalSteal()
    end
end
_G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot = function()
    local _Cl, _Dl = env.pcall(function()
        return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing")
    end
    )
    if _Cl and _Dl == true then
        return(2 > 1)
    end
    local _El = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _El then
        return(2 < 1)
    end
    local _Fl, _Gl = env.pcall(function()
        return _El:GetAttribute("Stealing")
    end
    )
    if _Fl and _Gl == true then
        return(2 > 1)
    end
    for _Hl, _Il in env.ipairs({"Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab"}) do
        local _Jl = _El:FindFirstChild(_Il, true)
        if _Jl then
            if _Jl:IsA("BoolValue") and _Jl.Value == true then
                return(2 > 1)
            end
            if _Jl:IsA("ObjectValue") and _Jl.Value ~= nil then
                return(0 < 1)
            end
            if _Jl:IsA("StringValue") and _Jl.Value ~= "" then
                return(2 > 1)
            end
        end
    end
    for _Kl, _Ll in env.ipairs(_El:GetChildren()) do
        if _Ll:IsA("Model") then
            local _Ml = env.string.lower(_Ll.Name)
            if(_Ml:find("brainrot", 1, true) or _Ml:find("animal", 1, true) or _Ml:find("carry", 1, true)) and _Ll:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",
            true) then
                return(1 == 1)
            end
        end
    end
    return(1 < 0)
end
_G.PrintedSafeModeIsLocked = function()
    if safeModeEnabled and _G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot() then
        return(2 > 1)
    end
    return(1 ~= 1)
end
_G.PrintedSafeModeForceStop = function()
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then
            autoLeftSetVisual(false)
        end
        if stopAutoLeft then
            env.pcall(stopAutoLeft)
        end
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then
            autoRightSetVisual(false)
        end
        if stopAutoRight then
            env.pcall(stopAutoRight)
        end
    end
    if autoBatEnabled then
        autoBatEnabled = false
        if autoBatSetVisual then
            autoBatSetVisual(false)
        end
        if disableAutoBat then
            env.pcall(disableAutoBat)
        end
    end
    if autoSwingEnabled then
        autoSwingEnabled = false
        if setAutoSwingVisual then
            setAutoSwingVisual(false)
        end
    end
end
_G.PrintedSafeModeTryStart = function(_Nl, _Ol)
    if _G.PrintedSafeModeIsLocked() then
        _G.PrintedSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
        if _G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot and _G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot() then
            if _G.PrintedSafeModeNotify then
                _G.PrintedSafeModeNotify(_Nl or "Holding brainrot")
            end
            if _Ol and _G.PrintedFlashBlockedButton then
                env.pcall(_G.PrintedFlashBlockedButton, _Ol)
            end
        end
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    return(2 > 1)
end
env.task.spawn(function()
    while env.task.wait(1.25) do
        if safeModeEnabled and _G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot and _G.PrintedSafeModeHoldingBrainrot() then
            if autoLeftEnabled or autoRightEnabled or autoBatEnabled then
                env.pcall(_G.PrintedSafeModeForceStop, "HOLDING BRAINROT")
            end
        end
    end
end
)
local _Pl = env.Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local function _Ql(_Rl)
    local _Sl = PrintedESP.data[_Rl]
    if not _Sl then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        if _Sl.highlight then
            _Sl.highlight:Destroy()
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        if _Sl.billboard then
            _Sl.billboard:Destroy()
        end
    end
    )
    for _Tl, _Ul in env.ipairs(_Sl.conns or {}) do
        env.pcall(function()
            _Ul:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    PrintedESP.data[_Rl] = nil
end
local function _Vl(_Wl, _Xl)
    if not PrintedESP.enabled or _Wl == LocalPlayer or not _Xl then
        return
    end
    _Ql(_Wl)
    local _Yl = _Xl:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or _Xl:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
    local _Zl = _Xl:FindFirstChild("Head") or _Xl:WaitForChild("Head", 5)
    if not _Yl or not _Zl or not PrintedESP.enabled then
        return
    end
    local _am = env.Instance.new("Highlight") _am.Name = "PrintedESP" _am.Adornee = _Xl _am.FillColor = env.Color3.fromRGB(255,
    140, 255) _am.FillTransparency = 0.35 _am.OutlineColor = env.Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) _am.OutlineTransparency = 0 _am.DepthMode = env.Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop _am.Parent = _Xl
    local _bm = env.Instance.new("BillboardGui") _bm.Name = "PrintedESPBillboard" _bm.Adornee = _Zl _bm.Size = env.UDim2.new(0,
    92, 0, 22) _bm.StudsOffset = env.Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0) _bm.AlwaysOnTop = true _bm.LightInfluence = 0 _bm.Parent = _Zl _bm.Size = env.UDim2.new(0,
    92, 0, 36) _bm.StudsOffset = env.Vector3.new(0, 2.6, 0)
    local _cm = env.Instance.new("TextLabel", _bm) _cm.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16) _cm.Position = env.UDim2.new(0,
    0, 0, 0) _cm.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _cm.TextColor3 = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1) _cm.Font = env.Enum.Font.GothamBold _cm.TextSize = 14 _cm.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3 _cm.Text = _Wl.DisplayName or _Wl.Name
    local _dm = env.Instance.new("TextLabel", _bm) _dm.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14) _dm.Position = env.UDim2.new(0,
    0, 0, 16) _dm.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _dm.TextColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(200, 210, 255) _dm.Font = env.Enum.Font.Gotham _dm.TextSize = 11 _dm.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2 _dm.Text = "0 speed"
    local _em = 0
    local _fm = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        if not PrintedESP.enabled or not _Yl.Parent then
            return
        end
        _em = _em + dt
        if _em < 0.1 then
            return
        end
        _em = 0
        local vel = _Yl.AssemblyLinearVelocity or _Yl.Velocity _dm.Text = env.string.format("%d speed", env.math.floor(env.Vector3.new(vel.X,
        0, vel.Z).Magnitude + 0.5))
    end
    ) PrintedESP.data[_Wl] = {highlight = _am, billboard = _bm, conns = {_fm}}
end
function startPrintedESP()
    if PrintedESP.enabled then
        return
    end
    PrintedESP.enabled = true
    for _gm, _hm in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if _hm ~= LocalPlayer then
            if _hm.Character then
                env.task.spawn(_Vl, _hm, _hm.Character)
            end
            env.table.insert(PrintedESP.conns, _hm.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(_im)
                env.task.defer(_Vl, _hm, _im)
            end
            ))
        end
    end
    env.table.insert(PrintedESP.conns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(_jm)
        if _jm == LocalPlayer then
            return
        end
        env.table.insert(PrintedESP.conns, _jm.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(_km)
            env.task.defer(_Vl, _jm, _km)
        end
        ))
    end
    )) env.table.insert(PrintedESP.conns, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(_Ql))
end
function stopPrintedESP()
    PrintedESP.enabled = false
    for _lm, _mm in env.ipairs(PrintedESP.conns) do
        env.pcall(function()
            _mm:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    PrintedESP.conns = {}
    for _nm in env.pairs(PrintedESP.data) do
        _Ql(_nm)
    end
    PrintedESP.data = {}
end
local _om = nil
local function _pm()
    if _om and _om.Parent then
        return _om
    end
    local _qm = env.Instance.new("ScreenGui") _qm.Name = "PrintedTracers" _qm.ResetOnSpawn = false _qm.IgnoreGuiInset = true _qm.DisplayOrder = env.tonumber("2000000") _qm.ZIndexBehavior = env.Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling env.pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(_qm)
        end
    end
    )
    local _rm = false env.pcall(function()
        if gethui then
            _qm.Parent = gethui() _rm = true
        end
    end
    )
    if not _rm then
        env.pcall(function()
            _qm.Parent = CoreGui _rm = true
        end
        )
    end
    if not _rm then
        env.pcall(function()
            _qm.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        end
        )
    end
    _om = _qm
    return _qm
end
local function _sm(_tm)
    local _um = PrintedTracer.data[_tm]
    if not _um then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _um:Destroy()
    end
    ) PrintedTracer.data[_tm] = nil
end
local function _vm()
    for _wm in env.pairs(PrintedTracer.data) do
        _sm(_wm)
    end
    PrintedTracer.data = {}
end
local function _xm()
    local _ym = env.Instance.new("Frame") _ym.Name = "PrintedTracerLine" _ym.BorderSizePixel = 0 _ym.BackgroundColor3 = _Pl _ym.BackgroundTransparency = 0 _ym.AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5,
    0.5) _ym.Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(0, 2) _ym.ZIndex = 10 _ym.Visible = false _ym.Parent = _pm()
    return _ym
end
local function _zm()
    if not PrintedTracer.enabled then
        return
    end
    local _Am = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not _Am then
        return
    end
    local _Bm = _pm()
    local _Cm = _Am.ViewportSize
    local _Dm = _Cm.X * 0.5
    local _Em = _Cm.Y - 60
    for _Fm, _Gm in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if _Gm ~= LocalPlayer then
            local _Hm = _Gm.Character
            if _Hm then
                _Hm = _Hm:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or _Hm:FindFirstChild("Head")
            end
            local _Im = PrintedTracer.data[_Gm]
            if not _Hm then
                if _Im then
                    _Im.Visible = false
                end
            else
                if not _Im or not _Im.Parent then
                    _Im = _xm() PrintedTracer.data[_Gm] = _Im
                elseif _Im.Parent ~= _Bm then
                    _Im.Parent = _Bm
                end
                local _Jm = _Am:WorldToViewportPoint(_Hm.Position - env.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                local _Km, _Lm = _Jm.X, _Jm.Y
                if _Jm.Z <= 0 then
                    _Km = _Cm.X * 0.5 - _Km - _Cm.X * 0.5
                    _Lm = _Cm.Y * 0.5 - _Lm - _Cm.Y * 0.5
                    local _Mm = _Km - _Cm.X * 0.5
                    local _Nm = _Lm - _Cm.Y * 0.5
                    local _Om = env.math.max(env.math.abs(_Mm), env.math.abs(_Nm))
                    if _Om > 0.001 then
                        local _Pm = env.math.max(_Cm.X, _Cm.Y) / _Om _Km = _Cm.X * 0.5 + _Mm * _Pm _Lm = _Cm.Y * 0.5 + _Nm * _Pm
                    end
                end
                local _Qm = env.math.clamp(_Km, 6, _Cm.X - 6)
                local _Rm = env.math.clamp(_Lm, 6, _Cm.Y - 6)
                local _Sm = _Qm - _Dm
                local _Tm = _Rm - _Em
                local _Um = env.math.sqrt(_Sm * _Sm + _Tm * _Tm)
                if _Um >= 2 then
                    _Im.Size = env.UDim2.fromOffset(env.math.floor(_Um), 2) _Im.Position = env.UDim2.fromOffset((_Dm + _Qm) * 0.5,
                    (_Em + _Rm) * 0.5) _Im.Rotation = env.math.deg(env.math.atan2(_Tm, _Sm)) _Im.BackgroundColor3 = _Pl _Im.BackgroundTransparency = 0 _Im.Visible = true
                else
                    _Im.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function startPrintedTracers()
    PrintedTracer.enabled = true _pm()
    if PrintedTracer.conn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedTracer.conn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
    end
    PrintedTracer.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        env.pcall(_zm)
    end
    )
end
function stopPrintedTracers()
    PrintedTracer.enabled = false
    if PrintedTracer.conn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedTracer.conn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedTracer.conn = nil
    end
    _vm()
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(_sm)
local _Vm, _Wm, _Xm = nil, nil, nil
local function _Ym(_Zm)
    env.pcall(function()
        if _Zm:IsA("Accessory") or _Zm:IsA("Hat") then
            _Zm:Destroy()
        elseif _Zm:IsA("BasePart") then
            _Zm.Material = env.Enum.Material.Plastic _Zm.Reflectance = 0 _Zm.CastShadow = false
        elseif _Zm:IsA("Decal") or _Zm:IsA("Texture") then
            _Zm.Transparency = 1
        elseif _Zm:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or _Zm:IsA("Trail") or _Zm:IsA("Beam") or _Zm:IsA("Fire") or _Zm:IsA("Smoke") or _Zm:IsA("Sparkles") then
            _Zm.Enabled = false
        elseif _Zm:IsA("AnimationController") or _Zm:IsA("Animator") then
            for _an, _bn in env.ipairs(_Zm:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                env.pcall(function()
                    _bn:Stop(0)
                end
                )
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
function enableAntiLag()
    removeAccessoriesEnabled = true
    antiLagEnabled = true
    if not _Vm then
        _Vm = Lighting.Brightness
    end
    _Wm = _Wm or Lighting.ClockTime _Xm = _Xm or Lighting.OutdoorAmbient Lighting.GlobalShadows = false Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10 Lighting.Brightness = 1 Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0 Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    for _cn, _dn in env.pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        env.pcall(function()
            if _dn:IsA("BlurEffect") or _dn:IsA("SunRaysEffect") or _dn:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or _dn:IsA("BloomEffect") or _dn:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                _dn.Enabled = false
            end
        end
        )
    end
    for _en, _fn in env.ipairs(env.workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        _Ym(_fn)
    end
    if antiLagDescConn then
        antiLagDescConn:Disconnect()
    end
    antiLagDescConn = env.workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(_gn)
        if removeAccessoriesEnabled then
            _Ym(_gn)
        end
    end
    )
end
function disableAntiLag()
    removeAccessoriesEnabled = false
    antiLagEnabled = false
    if antiLagDescConn then
        antiLagDescConn:Disconnect()
        antiLagDescConn = nil
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        if _Vm then
            Lighting.Brightness = _Vm
        end
        if _Wm then
            Lighting.ClockTime = _Wm
        end
        if _Xm then
            Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = _Xm
        end
        Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
    end
    )
end
local _hn = nil
function enableStretchRez()
    stretchRezEnabled = true
    if not env.workspace.CurrentCamera then
        return
    end
    if stretchRezConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            stretchRezConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        stretchRezConn = nil
    end
    if _hn then
        env.pcall(function()
            _hn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        _hn = nil
    end
    _hn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not stretchRezEnabled then
            return
        end
        if printedFOVEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _in = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
        if _in then
            env.pcall(function()
                _in.FieldOfView = stretchFOV or(518 % 0x8e + 28)
            end
            )
        end
    end
    )
    stretchRezConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not stretchRezEnabled then
            if stretchRezConn then
                stretchRezConn:Disconnect() stretchRezConn = nil
            end
            return
        end
        local _jn = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
        if _jn then
            _jn.CFrame = _jn.CFrame * env.CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0.7, 0, 0, 0, 1)
        end
    end
    )
end
function disableStretchRez()
    stretchRezEnabled = false
    if stretchRezConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            stretchRezConn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        stretchRezConn = nil
    end
    if _hn then
        env.pcall(function()
            _hn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        _hn = nil
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        local _kn = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
        if printedFOVEnabled then
            applyPrintedFOV()
        elseif _kn then
            _kn.FieldOfView = 70
        end
    end
    )
end
function applyPrintedFOV()
    local _ln = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not _ln then
        return
    end
    if printedFOVEnabled then
        _ln.FieldOfView = env.math.clamp(env.tonumber(printedFOVValue) or(0x2 * 35), 30, 120)
    end
end
local _mn = nil
function startPrintedFOV()
    printedFOVEnabled = true env.pcall(function()
        if _mn then
            if env.typeof(_mn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                _mn:Disconnect()
            end
            _mn = nil
        end
        env.pcall(function()
            RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("PrintedFOV")
        end
        )
    end
    )
    local function _nn()
        if not printedFOVEnabled then
            return
        end
        local _on = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
        if _on then
            _on.FieldOfView = env.math.clamp(env.tonumber(printedFOVValue) or(0xb * 6 + 4), 30, 120)
        end
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("PrintedFOV", env.Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, _nn)
    end
    )
    _mn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(_nn) applyPrintedFOV()
end
function stopPrintedFOV()
    printedFOVEnabled = false env.pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("PrintedFOV")
    end
    )
    if _mn then
        env.pcall(function()
            _mn:Disconnect()
        end
        )
        _mn = nil
    end
end

local function _qn(_rn, _sn)
    if not _rn or not _sn then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        if _rn:IsA("Animation") then
            _rn.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. env.tostring(_sn)
        elseif _rn:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation") then
            _rn:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation").AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. env.tostring(_sn)
        end
    end
    )
end
local function _tn(_un, ...)
    for _vn, _wn in env.ipairs({...}) do
        if _un[_wn] then
            return _un[_wn]
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function applyAnimPack(_xn)
    local _yn = _pn[_xn]
    if not _yn then
        return(2 < 1)
    end
    local _zn = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _zn then
        return(1 ~= 1)
    end
    local _An = _zn:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if not _An then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    local _Bn = _zn:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if _Bn then
        for _Cn, _Dn in env.ipairs(_Bn:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            env.pcall(function()
                _Dn:Stop(0)
            end
            )
        end
    end
    local function _En(_Fn, _Gn)
        if not _Fn then
            return nil
        end
        local _Hn = _Fn:FindFirstChild(_Gn)
        if not _Hn then
            _Hn = env.Instance.new("Animation") _Hn.Name = _Gn _Hn.Parent = _Fn
        end
        return _Hn
    end
    _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("walk"), "WalkAnim") or _An:FindFirstChild("walk"), _tn(_yn, "WalkAnim", "Walk")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("run"),
    "RunAnim") or _An:FindFirstChild("run"), _tn(_yn, "RunAnim", "Run")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("jump"), "JumpAnim") or _An:FindFirstChild("jump"),
    _tn(_yn, "JumpAnim", "Jump")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("fall"), "FallAnim") or _An:FindFirstChild("fall"), _tn(_yn,
    "FallAnim", "Fall")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("climb"), "ClimbAnim") or _An:FindFirstChild("climb"), _tn(_yn, "ClimbAnim",
    "Climb")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("swim"), "Swim") or _An:FindFirstChild("swim"), _tn(_yn, "Swim")) _qn(_En(_An:FindFirstChild("swimidle"),
    "SwimIdle") or _An:FindFirstChild("swimidle"), _tn(_yn, "SwimIdle") or _tn(_yn, "Swim"))
    local _In = _An:FindFirstChild("idle")
    if _In then
        local _Jn = _tn(_yn, "Animation1") or(_yn.Idle and _yn.Idle[1])
        local _Kn = _tn(_yn, "Animation2") or(_yn.Idle and _yn.Idle[2]) or _Jn _qn(_In:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), _Jn) _qn(_In:FindFirstChild("Animation2"),
        _Kn)
    end
    _An.Disabled = true env.task.wait(0.05) _An.Disabled = false
    animPack = _xn
    return(2 > 1)
end
function applyHeadlessToChar(_Ln, _Mn)
    if not _Ln then
        return
    end
    local _Nn = _Ln:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not _Nn then
        return
    end
    if _Mn then
        _Nn.Transparency = 1
        for _On, _Pn in env.ipairs(_Nn:GetChildren()) do
            if _Pn:IsA("Decal") or _Pn:IsA("Texture") then
                _Pn.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
        local _Qn = _Nn:FindFirstChild("face") or _Nn:FindFirstChild("Face")
        if _Qn then
            _Qn.Transparency = 1
        end
    else
        _Nn.Transparency = 0
        for _Rn, _Sn in env.ipairs(_Nn:GetChildren()) do
            if _Sn:IsA("Decal") or _Sn:IsA("Texture") then
                _Sn.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
    end
end
function applyKorbloxToChar(_Tn, _Un)
    if not _Tn then
        return
    end
    local _Vn = _Tn:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not _Vn then
        return
    end
    if _Un then
        if _Vn.RigType == env.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local _Wn = _Tn:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if _Wn then
                for _Xn, _Yn in env.ipairs(_Wn:GetChildren()) do
                    if _Yn:IsA("SpecialMesh") or _Yn:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                        _Yn:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                _Wn.Color = env.Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)
                local _Zn = env.Instance.new("SpecialMesh") _Zn.MeshType = env.Enum.MeshType.FileMesh _Zn.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696" _Zn.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254" _Zn.Scale = env.Vector3.new(1,
                1, 1) _Zn.Name = "KorbloxMesh" _Zn.Parent = _Wn
            end
        elseif _Vn.RigType == env.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local _ao = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if _ao then
                _ao.Transparency = 1
                local _bo = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local _co = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if _bo then
                    _bo.Transparency = 1
                end
                if _co then
                    _co.Transparency = 1
                end
                local _do = _Tn:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if _do then
                    _do:Destroy()
                end
                local _eo = env.Instance.new("Part") _eo.Name = "KorbloxLeg" _eo.Size = env.Vector3.new(1, 2, 1) _eo.Anchored = false _eo.CanCollide = false _eo.Color = env.Color3.fromRGB(64,
                64, 64) _eo.Parent = _Tn
                local _fo = env.Instance.new("SpecialMesh") _fo.MeshType = env.Enum.MeshType.FileMesh _fo.MeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696" _fo.TextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254" _fo.Scale = env.Vector3.new(1,
                1, 1) _fo.Name = "KorbloxMesh" _fo.Parent = _eo
                local _go = env.Instance.new("Weld") _go.Part0 = _ao _go.Part1 = _eo _go.C0 = env.CFrame.new(0, - 0.8, 0) _go.Name = "KorbloxWeld" _go.Parent = _eo
            end
        end
    else
        if _Vn.RigType == env.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local _ho = _Tn:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if _ho then
                for _io, _jo in env.ipairs(_ho:GetChildren()) do
                    if _jo:IsA("SpecialMesh") and _jo.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then
                        _jo:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif _Vn.RigType == env.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R16 then
        elseif _Vn.RigType == env.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local _ko = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if _ko then
                _ko.Transparency = 0
                local _lo = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local _mo = _Tn:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if _lo then
                    _lo.Transparency = 0
                end
                if _mo then
                    _mo.Transparency = 0
                end
                local _no = _Tn:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if _no then
                    _no:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
function applyCharterToChar(_oo)
    if not _oo then
        return
    end
    applyHeadlessToChar(_oo, headless) applyKorbloxToChar(_oo, korblox)
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(_po)
    env.task.defer(function()
        env.task.wait(0.6) env.pcall(applyCharterToChar, _po)
        if animPackEnabled then
            env.pcall(applyAnimPack, animPack)
        end
    end
    )
end
)
local _qo = nil
local _ro = 0
local _so = false
local function _to(_uo)
    if not _uo or not _uo.Parent then
        return
    end
    local _vo = _uo.MaxHealth or(11 * 9 + 0x1)
    if _vo <= 0 then
        _vo = 100
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        if _uo.MaxHealth < _vo then
            _uo.MaxHealth = _vo
        end
        _uo.Health = _vo
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(_uo, "Health", _vo) sethiddenproperty(_uo, "MaxHealth", _vo)
        end
    end
    ) PrintedAntiDie.invincibleUntil = env.tick() +(PrintedAntiDie.config.invincibilityFrames or 1.5) env.pcall(function()
        local _wo = _uo.Parent
        if not _wo then
            return
        end
        for _xo, _yo in env.ipairs(_wo:GetDescendants()) do
            if _yo:IsA("NumberValue") or _yo:IsA("IntValue") or _yo:IsA("DoubleConstrainedValue") then
                local _zo = _yo.Name:lower()
                if _zo:find("health") or _zo:find("hp") or _zo:find("life") then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _yo.Value = _vo
                    end
                    )
                end
            end
            if _yo:IsA("BoolValue") then
                local _Ao = _yo.Name:lower()
                if _Ao:find("dead") or _Ao:find("died") or _Ao:find("knocked") then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _yo.Value = false
                    end
                    )
                end
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
local function _Bo()
    if not PrintedAntiDie.config.autoRevive then
        return
    end
    local _Co = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Co then
        return
    end
    local _Do = _Co:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local _Eo = _Co:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Do then
        return
    end
    _to(_Do) env.pcall(function()
        _Do:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) _Do:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,
        false) _Do:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) _Do:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,
        false) _Do.BreakJointsOnDeath = false _Do:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) _Do:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics) _Do:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) _Do.PlatformStand = false _Do.Sit = false
    end
    )
    if _Eo then
        env.pcall(function()
            local _Fo = _Eo.AssemblyLinearVelocity _Eo.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.new(_Fo.X * 0.35, env.math.max(_Fo.Y,
            - 8) * 0.35, _Fo.Z * 0.35) _Eo.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _Eo.AssemblyAngularVelocity * 0
        end
        )
    end
end
local function _Go(_Ho)
    if not _Ho then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _Ho.BreakJointsOnDeath = false _Ho.RequiresNeck = false _Ho:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) _Ho:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown,
        false) _Ho:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) _Ho:SetStateEnabled(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics,
        false)
    end
    )
end
local function _Io()
    local _Jo = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Jo then
        return
    end
    local _Ko = _Jo:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local _Lo = _Jo:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Ko or not _Lo then
        return
    end
    if _Ko.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    if _Ko:GetState() == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall and _Lo.Position.Y < - 80 then
        return
    end
    if _Lo.Position.Y < - 50 then
        return
    end
    if env.tick() - _ro < 0.2 then
        return
    end
    _ro = env.tick()
    _qo = _Lo.CFrame
end
local function _Mo(_No)
    if not _No then
        return
    end
    if _No.Position.Y > - 120 then
        return
    end
    if not _qo then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        _No.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _No.CFrame = _qo + env.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    end
    )
end
function startAntiDie()
    antiDieEnabled = true PrintedAntiDie.enabled = true
    if PrintedAntiDie.loop then
        PrintedAntiDie.loop:Disconnect() PrintedAntiDie.loop = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.healthConn then
        PrintedAntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect() PrintedAntiDie.healthConn = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.diedConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.diedConn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.diedConn = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.removeConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.removeConn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.removeConn = nil
    end
    local _Oo = 0 PrintedAntiDie.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
            return
        end
        local _Po = LocalPlayer.Character
        if _Po then
            local _Qo = _Po:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local _Ro = _Po:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not _Qo then
                return
            end
            if env.tick() - _Oo > 0.5 then
                _Oo = env.tick() _Go(_Qo)
            end
            _Mo(_Ro)
            if _Qo.Health <= 0 then
                _Bo()
            else
                _Io()
                if _Qo.Health <(_Qo.MaxHealth or(259 % 67 + 0x2a)) then
                    _to(_Qo)
                end
                if env.tick() <(PrintedAntiDie.invincibleUntil or(6 - 0x6)) then
                    if _Qo.Health <(_Qo.MaxHealth or(0x52 + 18)) then
                        _Qo.Health = _Qo.MaxHealth or(235 % 0xac + 0x25)
                    end
                end
            end
            return
        end
    end
    ) PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
            return
        end
        local _So = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _To = _So and _So:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _To then
            return
        end
        if _To.Health <(_To.MaxHealth or(0xa86 - 2594)) then
            _to(_To)
        end
        local _Uo = _To:GetState()
        if _Uo == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or _Uo == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or _Uo == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics then
            _Go(_To) env.pcall(function()
                _To:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) _To:ChangeState(env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) _To.PlatformStand = false
            end
            )
        end
    end
    )
    local function _Vo(_Wo)
        if PrintedAntiDie.healthConn then
            PrintedAntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect() PrintedAntiDie.healthConn = nil
        end
        if PrintedAntiDie.diedConn then
            env.pcall(function()
                PrintedAntiDie.diedConn:Disconnect()
            end
            ) PrintedAntiDie.diedConn = nil
        end
        if PrintedAntiDie.removeConn then
            env.pcall(function()
                PrintedAntiDie.removeConn:Disconnect()
            end
            ) PrintedAntiDie.removeConn = nil
        end
        local _Xo = _Wo and _Wo:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or _Wo and _Wo:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not _Xo then
            return
        end
        _Go(_Xo) PrintedAntiDie.healthConn = _Xo.HealthChanged:Connect(function(_Yo)
            if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
                return
            end
            if _Yo <(_Xo.MaxHealth or(0x5 + 0x2f + 0x30)) then
                _to(_Xo)
            end
            if _Yo <= 0 then
                _Bo() env.task.defer(_Bo)
            end
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.diedConn = _Xo.Died:Connect(function()
            if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
                return
            end
            _so = true _Bo() env.task.defer(_Bo)
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.removeConn = _Wo.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_Zo, _ap)
            if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
                return
            end
            if _ap == nil then
                _so = true
            end
        end
        ) _to(_Xo)
    end
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        _Vo(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.charConn then
        PrintedAntiDie.charConn:Disconnect()
    end
    PrintedAntiDie.charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(_bp)
        if not PrintedAntiDie.enabled then
            return
        end
        env.task.wait(0.03) _Vo(_bp)
        local _cp = _bp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if _cp then
            _to(_cp) _Go(_cp)
        end
        if _so and _qo then
            _so = false
            local _dp = _bp:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or _bp:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if _dp then
                for _ep = 1, 6 do
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _dp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _dp.CFrame = _qo + env.Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                    end
                    ) env.task.wait(0.08)
                end
            end
        else
            _so = false
        end
    end
    )
end
function stopAntiDie()
    antiDieEnabled = false PrintedAntiDie.enabled = false
    if PrintedAntiDie.loop then
        PrintedAntiDie.loop:Disconnect() PrintedAntiDie.loop = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.stepLoop = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.healthConn then
        PrintedAntiDie.healthConn:Disconnect() PrintedAntiDie.healthConn = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.diedConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.diedConn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.diedConn = nil
    end
    if PrintedAntiDie.removeConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedAntiDie.removeConn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedAntiDie.removeConn = nil
    end
    _so = false
end
local function _fp()
    local _gp = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
    for _hp, _ip in env.ipairs({"sethiddenproperty", "set_hidden_property", "sethiddenprop", "set_hidden_prop"}) do
        local _jp = env.rawget(_gp, _ip)
        if type(_jp) == "function" then
            return _jp
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function _kp()
    local _lp = getgenv and getgenv() or _G
    for _mp, _np in env.ipairs({"gethiddenproperty", "get_hidden_property", "gethiddenprop", "get_hidden_prop"}) do
        local _op = env.rawget(_lp, _np)
        if type(_op) == "function" then
            return _op
        end
    end
    return nil
end
local function _pp(_qp, _rp, _sp)
    if not _qp then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    local _tp = _fp()
    if _tp then
        if env.pcall(_tp, _qp, _rp, _sp) then
            return(1 == 1)
        end
    end
    return env.pcall(function()
        _qp[_rp] = _sp
    end
    )
end
local function _up(_vp, _wp)
    if not _vp then
        return(1 < 0), nil
    end
    local _xp = _kp()
    if _xp then
        local _yp, _zp = env.pcall(_xp, _vp, _wp)
        if _yp then
            return(1 == 1), _zp
        end
    end
    local _Ap, _Bp = env.pcall(function()
        return _vp[_wp]
    end
    )
    return _Ap, _Bp
end
local function _Cp(_Dp)
    if not _Dp then
        return(1 ~= 1)
    end
    local _Ep, _Fp = env.pcall(function()
        return _Dp:IsA("BasePart")
    end
    )
    return _Ep and _Fp == true
end
local function _Gp()
    local _Hp = PrintedDesync.fakeRoot PrintedDesync.fakeRoot = nil
    if _Hp then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Hp:Destroy()
        end
        )
    end
end
local function _Ip()
    local _Jp = PrintedDesync.repRootOwner
    if _Cp(_Jp) then
        _pp(_Jp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Jp)
    end
    PrintedDesync.repRootOwner = nil
end
local function _Kp(_Lp)
    _Gp()
    local _Mp = env.Instance.new("Part") _Mp.Name = PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_NAME _Mp.Size = env.Vector3.new(2, 2, 1) _Mp.Anchored = true _Mp.CanCollide = false _Mp.CanTouch = false _Mp.CanQuery = false _Mp.Transparency = 1 _Mp.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(0,
    PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_Y, 0) _Mp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_VELOCITY _Mp.Parent = env.workspace env.pcall(function()
        local _Np = _Lp.Position _Mp.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_Np.X, PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_Y, _Np.Z)
    end
    ) PrintedDesync.fakeRoot = _Mp
    return _Mp
end
local function _Op(_Pp, _Qp)
    if not _Cp(_Pp) or not _Cp(_Qp) then
        return(2 < 1)
    end
    _pp(_Pp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Pp) PrintedDesync.repRootOwner = _Pp
    return _pp(_Pp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Qp)
end
local function _Rp()
    if not PrintedDesync.alive or not PrintedDesync.enabled then
        return
    end
    local _Sp = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _Tp = _Sp and _Sp:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Cp(_Tp) then
        return
    end
    local _Up = PrintedDesync.fakeRoot
    if not _Cp(_Up) or not _Up.Parent then
        local _Vp = _Kp(_Tp) _Op(_Tp, _Vp)
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        local _Wp = _Tp.Position
        local _Xp = _Up.Position
        local _Yp = env.math.abs(_Wp.X - _Xp.X) > 0.01 or env.math.abs(_Wp.Z - _Xp.Z) > 0.01
        if not _Yp then
            _Yp = env.math.abs(_Xp.Y - PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_Y) > 0.1
        end
        if _Yp then
            _Up.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_Wp.X, PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_Y, _Wp.Z)
        end
        _Up.Anchored = true _Up.AssemblyLinearVelocity = PrintedDesync.FAKE_ROOT_VELOCITY
    end
    )
    local _Zp, _aq = _up(_Tp, "PhysicsRepRootPart")
    if not _Zp or _aq ~= _Up then
        _pp(_Tp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Up)
    end
end
local function _bq()
    if PrintedDesync.stepConnection then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedDesync.stepConnection:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedDesync.stepConnection = nil
    end
end
local function _cq()
    _bq() PrintedDesync.stepConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(_Rp)
end
local function _dq()
    if PrintedDesync.antiBatConn then
        env.pcall(function()
            PrintedDesync.antiBatConn:Disconnect()
        end
        ) PrintedDesync.antiBatConn = nil
    end
    PrintedDesync.lastSafeCFrame = nil
end
local function _eq()
    _dq() PrintedDesync.lastSafeCFrame = nil PrintedDesync.lastCheckTime = 0 PrintedDesync.antiBatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not PrintedDesync.enabled or not PrintedDesync.alive then
            return
        end
        local _fq = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not _fq then
            return
        end
        local _gq = _fq:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local _hq = _fq:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not _gq or not _hq or _hq.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end
        local _iq = env.tick()
        local _jq = _gq.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if _jq.Magnitude < 70 then
            PrintedDesync.lastSafeCFrame = _gq.CFrame PrintedDesync.lastCheckTime = _iq
        elseif _jq.Magnitude > 110 and PrintedDesync.lastSafeCFrame and _iq - PrintedDesync.lastCheckTime < 0.5 then
            _gq.CFrame = PrintedDesync.lastSafeCFrame * env.CFrame.new(0, 0.1, 0)
        end
        for _kq, _lq in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if _lq ~= LocalPlayer and _lq.Character then
                local _mq = _lq.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local _nq = _lq.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
                if _mq and _nq and _nq.Name:lower():find("bat") then
                    if(_gq.Position - _mq.Position).Magnitude < PrintedDesync.ANTI_BAT_RANGE then
                        local _oq = env.math.rad(env.tick() * 500) _gq.CFrame = _gq.CFrame * env.CFrame.new(env.math.sin(_oq) * 3, 0,
                        env.math.cos(_oq) * 3)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
function startPrintedDesync()
    PrintedDesync.enabled = true _pp(LocalPlayer, "MaximumSimulationRadius", env.math.huge) _pp(LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius",
    env.math.huge) env.pcall(function()
        settings().Network.InterpolationThrottling = env.Enum.InterpolationThrottlingMode.Disabled
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        local _pq = settings().Physics _pq.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = env.Enum.EnviromentalPhysicsThrottle.Disabled _pq.AllowSleep = false
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        env.game:GetService("NetworkClient"):SetOutgoingKBPSLimit(env.math.huge)
    end
    )
    local _qq = LocalPlayer.Character _qq = _qq and _qq:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if _qq then
        local _rq = _Kp(_qq) _Op(_qq, _rq) _cq()
    end
    _eq()
end
function stopPrintedDesync()
    PrintedDesync.enabled = false _bq() _dq() _Ip() _Gp()
end
local function _sq()
    local _tq = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _tq then
        return nil
    end
    local _uq = _tq:FindFirstChild("Bat")
    if _uq then
        return _uq
    end
    local _vq = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if _vq then
        local _wq = _vq:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if _wq then
            _wq.Parent = _tq
            return _wq
        end
    end
    return nil
end
function tryHitBatG12()
    if tpBatHittingCD then
        return
    end
    tpBatHittingCD = true env.pcall(function()
        local _xq = _sq()
        if _xq then
            _xq:Activate()
            local _yq = _xq:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if _yq then
                _yq:FireServer()
            end
        end
    end
    ) env.task.delay(0.08, function()
        tpBatHittingCD = false
    end
    )
end
local function _zq()
    local _Aq = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _Bq = _Aq and _Aq:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _Bq then
        return nil, env.math.huge
    end
    local _Cq, _Dq = nil, env.math.huge
    for _Eq, _Fq in env.ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if _Fq ~= LocalPlayer and _Fq.Character then
            local _Gq = _Fq.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if _Gq then
                local _Hq =(_Bq.Position - _Gq.Position).Magnitude
                if _Hq < _Dq then
                    _Dq = _Hq _Cq = _Fq
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return _Cq, _Dq
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not tpBatEnabled then
        return
    end
    local _Iq = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _Iq then
        return
    end
    local _Jq = _Iq:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local _Kq = _Iq:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not _Jq or not _Kq or _Kq.Health <= 0 then
        return
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        local _Lq = _Iq:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        local _Mq = not _Lq
        if not _Mq then
            local _Nq = env.tostring(_Lq.Name):lower() _Mq = not(_Nq:find("bat") or _Nq:find("slap"))
        end
        if _Mq then
            local _Oq = _sq()
            if not _Oq then
                local _Pq = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                for _Qq, _Rq in env.ipairs(_qh) do
                    _Oq = _Iq:FindFirstChild(_Rq) or(_Pq and _Pq:FindFirstChild(_Rq))
                    if _Oq then
                        break
                    end
                end
                if not _Oq and _Pq then
                    for _Sq, _Tq in env.ipairs(_Pq:GetChildren()) do
                        if _Tq:IsA("Tool") then
                            local _Uq = _Tq.Name:lower()
                            if _Uq:find("bat") or _Uq:find("slap") then
                                _Oq = _Tq
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if _Oq and _Oq.Parent ~= _Iq then
                _Kq:EquipTool(_Oq)
            end
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        for _Vq, _Wq in env.ipairs(env.workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if _Wq.Name == "FallDamage" or(_Wq:IsA("Model") and _Wq.Name:find("Fall")) then
                _Wq:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        if sethiddenproperty then
            sethiddenproperty(_Jq, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Jq) sethiddenproperty(LocalPlayer, "SimulationRadius", env.math.huge) sethiddenproperty(LocalPlayer,
            "MaximumSimulationRadius", env.math.huge)
        end
    end
    )
    local _Xq = _zq()
    if _Xq and _Xq.Character then
        local _Yq = _Xq.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if _Yq then
            if sethiddenproperty then
                env.pcall(function()
                    sethiddenproperty(_Jq, "PhysicsRepRootPart", _Yq)
                end
                )
            end
            local _Zq = _Yq.Position + env.Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
            if(_Jq.Position - _Zq).Magnitude > 6 then
                _Jq.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_Zq) env.pcall(function()
                    _Jq.AssemblyLinearVelocity = env.Vector3.zero _Jq.AssemblyAngularVelocity = env.Vector3.zero
                end
                )
            end
            if tpBatCamShake then
                local _ar = env.workspace.CurrentCamera
                if _ar then
                    env.pcall(function()
                        _ar.CFrame = env.CFrame.new(_ar.CFrame.Position, _Yq.Position)
                    end
                    )
                end
            end
            if tpBatAutoSwing ~= false then
                tryHitBatG12()
            end
        end
    end
end
)
local _br = env.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
local _cr = {[env.Enum.KeyCode.W] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.A] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.S] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.D] = true,
[env.Enum.KeyCode.Up] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.Left] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.Down] = true,[env.Enum.KeyCode.Right] = true,
} RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
    local _dr = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not _dr then
        return
    end
    local _er = _dr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local _fr = _dr:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not _er or not _fr then
        return
    end
    if _Zb(_er) then
        _br = env.Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        return
    end
    if not autoBatEnabled and not autoLeftEnabled and not autoRightEnabled then
        local _gr = _er.MoveDirection
        local _hr = _Xb()
        if _gr.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            _br = _gr _ec(_fr, _er, _hr, _gr)
        elseif antiRagdollEnabled and _br.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            local _ir = false
            for _jr in env.pairs(_cr) do
                if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(_jr) then
                    _ir = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if _ir then
                _ec(_fr, _er, _hr, _br)
            else
                _ec(_fr, _er, _hr, env.Vector3.zero)
            end
        else
            _ec(_fr, _er, _hr, env.Vector3.zero)
        end
    end
    if speedLabel then
        local _kr = _fr.AssemblyLinearVelocity or _fr.Velocity speedLabel.Text = env.string.format("Speed: %.1f", env.Vector3.new(_kr.X,
        0, _kr.Z).Magnitude)
    end
end
)
local _lr = false
local _mr = nil
local _nr = 0
local _or = false
local function _pr()
    if refreshSpeedModeLabel then
        env.pcall(refreshSpeedModeLabel)
    end
end
local function _qr(_rr)
    if _lr then
        return
    end
    if laggerToggled and laggerPhase == 2 then
        _mr = "LaggerCarry"
    elseif laggerToggled then
        _mr = "Lagger"
    elseif speedMode then
        _mr = "Carry"
    else
        _mr = "Normal"
    end
    _lr = true
    _nr = env.tick() + 0.75
    if _mr == "Lagger" or _mr == "LaggerCarry" then
        laggerToggled = true
        laggerPhase = 1
        speedMode = false
    else
        laggerToggled = false
        laggerPhase = 0
        speedMode = true
    end
    _pr()
end
local function _sr()
    if not _lr then
        return
    end
    _lr = false
    _nr = 0
    local _tr = _mr _mr = nil
    if _tr == "Lagger" then
        laggerToggled = true
        laggerPhase = 1
        speedMode = false
    elseif _tr == "LaggerCarry" then
        laggerToggled = true
        laggerPhase = 2
        speedMode = false
    elseif _tr == "Carry" then
        laggerToggled = false
        laggerPhase = 0
        speedMode = true
    else
        laggerToggled = false
        laggerPhase = 0
        speedMode = false
    end
    _pr()
end
local function _ur(_vr)
    if not _vr then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    for _wr, _xr in env.ipairs({"Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab"}) do
        local _yr = _vr:FindFirstChild(_xr, true)
        if _yr then
            if _yr:IsA("BoolValue") and _yr.Value then
                return(1 == 1)
            end
            if _yr:IsA("ObjectValue") and _yr.Value then
                return(2 > 1)
            end
            if _yr:IsA("StringValue") and _yr.Value ~= "" then
                return(2 > 1)
            end
        end
    end
    for _zr, _Ar in env.ipairs(_vr:GetChildren()) do
        if _Ar:IsA("Model") and _Ar:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
            if _Ar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") and _Ar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                return(1 == 1)
            end
        elseif _Ar:IsA("Tool") then
            local _Br = _Ar.Name:lower()
            if not(_Br:find("bat") or _Br:find("slap") or _Br:find("medusa")) then
                return(0 < 1)
            end
        end
    end
    return(1 < 0)
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if autoCarryEnabled ~= true then
        if _lr then
            _sr()
        end
        _or = false
        return
    end
    local _Cr = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _Dr = _Cr and _Cr:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not _Cr or not _Dr then
        if _lr then
            _sr()
        end
        _or = false
        return
    end
    local _Er = _Dr:GetState()
    local _Fr = _Er == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or _Er == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or _Er == env.Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    local _Gr = false env.pcall(function()
        _Gr = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    end
    )
    if not _Gr then
        env.pcall(function()
            local _Hr = LocalPlayer.Character
            if _Hr then
                _Gr = _Hr:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
            end
        end
        )
    end
    local _Ir = _ur(_Cr)
    if _Gr and not _or then
        _or = true _qr()
    elseif not _Gr then
        _or = false
    end
    if _Ir and not _lr then
        _qr()
    end
    if _lr then
        if _Fr or(env.tick() > _nr and not _Ir and not _Gr) then
            _sr()
        end
    end
end
)
INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS = {}
for _Jr = 1, 10 do
    env.table.insert(INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS, {name = "Track " .. _Jr})
end
function getIntroMusicName(_Kr)
    local _Lr = INTRO_MUSIC_OPTIONS[_Kr or selectedIntroMusic]
    _Lr = _Lr and _Lr.name
    if not _Lr then
        _Lr = "Track " .. env.tostring(_Kr or(3 - 0x2))
    end
    return _Lr
end
stopIntroPreview = function()
    local _Mr = _currentIntroSound _currentIntroSound = nil
    if _Mr then
        env.pcall(function()
            _Mr:Stop()
        end
        ) env.pcall(function()
            _Mr.Volume = 0
        end
        ) env.pcall(function()
            _Mr:Destroy()
        end
        )
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        local _Nr = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if _Nr then
            local _Or = _Nr:FindFirstChild("PrintedIntroMusic")
            if _Or then
                env.pcall(function()
                    _Or:Destroy()
                end
                )
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
stopIntroPlayback = function()
    stopIntroPreview() env.pcall(function()
        local _Pr = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if _Pr then
            local _Qr = _Pr:FindFirstChild("PrintedIntro")
            if _Qr then
                _Qr:Destroy()
            end
        end
        if gethui then
            local _Rr = gethui()
            local _Sr = _Rr and _Rr:FindFirstChild("PrintedIntro")
            if _Sr then
                _Sr:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    )
end
_G.PrintedStopIntroMusic = stopIntroPlayback _safeNotify = function(_Tr)
    if _G.PrintedSafeModeNotify then
        env.pcall(_G.PrintedSafeModeNotify, _Tr)
    end
end
local function _Ur(_Vr)
    local _Wr = _Vr or(INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS and INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[selectedIntroMusic or(0xe - 13)])
    if not _Wr then
        return nil
    end
    local _Xr = writefile or(syn and syn.writefile)
    local _Yr = getcustomasset or getsynasset or(syn and syn.getcustomasset)
    local _Zr = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or fluxus and fluxus.request or request or http_request
    local _as = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
    if not _as then
        return nil
    end
    env.pcall(function()
        local _bs = _as:FindFirstChild("PrintedIntroMusic")
        if _bs then
            _bs:Destroy()
        end
    end
    )
    local _cs = env.Instance.new("Sound") _cs.Name = "PrintedIntroMusic" _cs.Volume = 3 _cs.Parent = _as _cs.Looped = false _cs.PlayOnRemove = false
    _currentIntroSound = _cs
    local _ds = env.tonumber(selectedIntroMusic) or(0xe - 13) env.task.spawn(function()
        local _es = {env.tostring(_Wr):match("%.([%w]+)$") or "mp3", "mp3", "ogg", "wav"}
        local _fs = nil
        if type(_Zr) == "function" and type(_Yr) == "function" and type(_Xr) == "function" then
            local _gs, _hs = env.pcall(_Zr, {Url = _Wr, Method = "GET"})
            local _is = _gs and _hs and(_hs.Body or _hs.body)
            if type(_is) == "string" and # _is > 0 then
                for _js, _ks in env.ipairs(_es) do
                    local _ls = "printed_intro_" .. env.tostring(_ds) .. "." .. _ks
                    if env.pcall(_Xr, _ls, _is) then
                        local _ms, _ns = env.pcall(_Yr, _ls)
                        if _ms and type(_ns) == "string" and # _ns > 0 then
                            _fs = _ns
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not _fs and type(_Yr) == "function" and type(_Xr) == "function" then
            local _os, _ps = env.pcall(function()
                return env.game:HttpGet(_Wr)
            end
            )
            if _os and type(_ps) == "string" and # _ps > 500 then
                for _qs, _rs in env.ipairs(_es) do
                    local _ss = "printed_intro_" .. env.tostring(_ds) .. "." .. _rs
                    if env.pcall(_Xr, _ss, _ps) then
                        local _ts, _us = env.pcall(_Yr, _ss)
                        if _ts and type(_us) == "string" and # _us > 0 then
                            _fs = _us
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not _cs.Parent then
            return
        end
        if not _fs then
            env.warn("[Printed] Intro audio could not be converted to a local asset: " .. env.tostring(_Wr))
            return
        end
        _cs.SoundId = _fs env.pcall(function()
            ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({_cs})
        end
        )
        if _cs.Parent then
            env.pcall(function()
                _cs:Play()
            end
            )
        end
        env.task.wait(1)
        if _cs and _cs.Parent and not _cs.IsPlaying then
            env.pcall(function()
                _cs:Play()
            end
            )
        end
    end
    ) env.task.delay(15, function()
        env.pcall(function()
            if _cs and _cs.Parent then
                _cs:Stop() _cs:Destroy()
            end
        end
        )
        if _currentIntroSound == _cs then
            _currentIntroSound = nil
        end
    end
    )
    return _cs
end
getIntroAsset = function(_vs)
    return {audio = nil, video = INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[env.tonumber(_vs) or selectedIntroMusic or(17 - 16)], path = "printed_intro_track.mp3"}
end
_Ur = function(_ws)
    return _Ur(INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[env.tonumber(_ws) or selectedIntroMusic or(0x3 - 2)])
end
previewIntroMusic = function(_xs)
    env.task.spawn(function()
        env.pcall(function()
            stopIntroPreview() _Ur(INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[env.tonumber(_xs) or selectedIntroMusic or(13 - 12)])
        end
        )
    end
    )
end
playIntroSequence = function(_ys)
    stopIntroPlayback() env.task.spawn(function()
        local _zs = INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[selectedIntroMusic or(14 - 13)] or INTRO_MUSIC_LINKS[1] env.pcall(function()
            _Ur(_zs)
        end
        )
        local _As = env.Instance.new("ScreenGui") _As.Name = "PrintedIntro" _As.ResetOnSpawn = false _As.IgnoreGuiInset = true _As.DisplayOrder = env.tonumber("100000") _As.ZIndexBehavior = env.Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        local _Bs = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        local _Cs = false env.pcall(function()
            if gethui then
                _As.Parent = gethui() _Cs = true
            end
        end
        )
        if not _Cs then
            _As.Parent = _Bs
        end
        local _Ds = env.tick()
        local function _Es(_Fs)
            local _Gs = env.tick() - _Ds
            local _Hs = _Fs - _Gs
            if _Hs > 0 then
                env.task.wait(_Hs)
            end
        end
        local _Is = env.Color3.fromRGB(90, 150, 255)
        local _Js = env.Instance.new("Frame", _As) _Js.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) _Js.BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(6,
        8, 14) _Js.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _Js.BorderSizePixel = 0
        local _Ks = env.Instance.new("Frame", _As) _Ks.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) _Ks.BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(0,
        0, 0) _Ks.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _Ks.ZIndex = 2
        local _Ls = env.Instance.new("Frame", _As) _Ls.AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5) _Ls.Position = env.UDim2.new(0.5,
        0, 0.48, 0) _Ls.Size = env.UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0, 2) _Ls.BackgroundColor3 = _Is _Ls.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        local _Ms = env.Instance.new("TextLabel", _As) _Ms.AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5) _Ms.Position = env.UDim2.new(0.5,
        0, 0.48, 0) _Ms.Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 520, 0, 70) _Ms.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _Ms.Text = "PRINTED" _Ms.Font = env.Enum.Font.GothamBlack _Ms.TextSize = 68 _Ms.TextColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(245,
        248, 255) _Ms.TextTransparency = 1 _Ms.TextStrokeColor3 = _Is _Ms.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
        local _Ns = env.Instance.new("TextLabel", _As) _Ns.AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0.5, 0) _Ns.Position = env.UDim2.new(0.5,
        0, 0.48, 48) _Ns.Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 22) _Ns.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _Ns.Text = "deobf by discord.gg/speedhub" _Ns.Font = env.Enum.Font.GothamMedium _Ns.TextSize = 14 _Ns.TextColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(180,
        190, 255) _Ns.TextTransparency = 1
        local _Os = env.Instance.new("BlurEffect") _Os.Size = 0 _Os.Parent = Lighting TweenService:Create(_Os, env.TweenInfo.new(0.3),
        {Size = 14}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Js, env.TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.08}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Ks,
        env.TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.35}):Play() _Es(0.3) TweenService:Create(_Ms, env.TweenInfo.new(0.4,
        env.Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, env.Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {TextTransparency = 0, TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Ls,
        env.TweenInfo.new(0.45, env.Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, env.Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = env.UDim2.new(0,
        200, 0, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 0}):Play() env.task.wait(0.15) TweenService:Create(_Ns, env.TweenInfo.new(0.3),
        {TextTransparency = 0}):Play() _Es(2.1) TweenService:Create(_Ms, env.TweenInfo.new(0.15), {TextTransparency = 1,
        TextStrokeTransparency = 1}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Ns, env.TweenInfo.new(0.3), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Ls,
        env.TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 2)}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Js,
        env.TweenInfo.new(0.35), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Ks, env.TweenInfo.new(0.45),
        {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() TweenService:Create(_Os, env.TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = 0}):Play() _Es(3.5) env.pcall(function()
            _As:Destroy()
        end
        ) env.pcall(function()
            _Os:Destroy()
        end
        )
        if _ys then
            env.pcall(_ys)
        end
    end
    )
end
function createMobilePanel()
    env.pcall(function()
        local _Ps = {CoreGui, LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"), gethui and gethui()}
        for _Qs, _Rs in env.ipairs(_Ps) do
            if _Rs then
                for _Ss, _Ts in env.ipairs({"PrintedMobileButtons", "PrintedMobileButtons2"}) do
                    local _Us = _Rs:FindFirstChild(_Ts)
                    if _Us then
                        _Us:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        local _Vs = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("PrintedMobileButtons")
        if _Vs then
            _Vs:Destroy()
        end
    end
    ) env.pcall(function()
        local _Ws = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if _Ws then
            local _Xs = _Ws:FindFirstChild("PrintedMobileButtons")
            if _Xs then
                _Xs:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

        if syn and syn.protect_gui then
            syn.protect_gui(_Ys)
        end
    end
    )

        end
