    local _ct = env.Instance.new("Frame", _at) _ct.Name = "EditBanner" _ct.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26) _ct.Position = env.UDim2.new(0,
    0, 0, - 32) _ct.BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 255) _ct.BorderSizePixel = 0 _ct.Visible = false _ct.ZIndex = 51 env.Instance.new("UICorner",

    15, 18) _dt.Font = env.Enum.Font.GothamBlack _dt.TextSize = 12 _dt.ZIndex = 11

        20)

        140, 255)

        env.Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 255)),})

        255, 255) _qt.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _qt.BorderSizePixel = 0 _qt.ZIndex = 1 env.Instance.new("UICorner", _qt).CornerRadius = env.UDim.new(0,
        18)
        local _rt = env.Instance.new("UIGradient", _qt)
        local _st = _Dc()
        if _st and _st.grad then
            _rt.Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, _st.grad[1]), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45,
            _st.grad[2]), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, _st.grad[4]),})
        else
            _rt.Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, env.Color3.fromRGB(80, 160, 255)), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45,
            env.Color3.fromRGB(40, 150, 255)), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, env.Color3.fromRGB(12, 14, 22)),})
        end
        _rt.Rotation = 45
        local _tt = env.Instance.new("UIStroke", _lt) _tt.Color = env.Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65) _tt.Thickness = 1.4 _tt.Transparency = 0.25
        local _ut = env.Instance.new("UIGradient", _tt) _ut.Color = env.ColorSequence.new({env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,
        env.Color3.fromRGB(120, 210, 255)), env.ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, env.Color3.fromRGB(130, 32, 130)),})
        local _vt = env.Instance.new("Frame", _lt) _vt.Name = "FlashLayer" _vt.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) _vt.BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(255,
        140, 255) _vt.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _vt.BorderSizePixel = 0 _vt.ZIndex = 2 env.Instance.new("UICorner", _vt).CornerRadius = env.UDim.new(0,
        18)
        local _wt = env.Instance.new("TextLabel", _lt) _wt.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, - 6, 1, - 6) _wt.Position = env.UDim2.new(0,
        3, 0, 3) _wt.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _wt.Text = _ht _wt.TextColor3 = env.Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240) _wt.Font = env.Enum.Font.GothamBlack _wt.TextSize = 14 _wt.TextWrapped = true _wt.TextScaled = false
        local _xt = env.Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint", _wt) _xt.MinTextSize = 10 _xt.MaxTextSize = 15 _wt.TextXAlignment = env.Enum.TextXAlignment.Center _wt.TextYAlignment = env.Enum.TextYAlignment.Center _wt.ZIndex = 3
        local _yt = env.Instance.new("TextButton", _lt) _yt.Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) _yt.BackgroundTransparency = 1 _yt.Text = "" _yt.AutoButtonColor = false _yt.ZIndex = 6 _yt.Active = true _yt.Selectable = false
        _printedBtnSeq = _printedBtnSeq + 1
        local _zt = env.UDim2.new(0,(_it or(0x11 - 17)), 0,(_jt or(3 - 0x3)))
        local _At = mobileButtonPositions[_gt]
        local _Bt = false
        if type(_At) == "table" then
            local _Ct = env.tonumber(_At.x) or(0xa - 10)
            local _Dt = env.tonumber(_At.y) or(16 - 16)
            local _Et = env.tonumber(_At.xs) or(10 - 0xa)
            local _Ft = env.tonumber(_At.ys) or(7 - 7)
            if env.math.abs(_Ct) <= 2000 and env.math.abs(_Dt) <= 2000 and env.math.abs(_Et) <= 1 and env.math.abs(_Ft) <= 1 then
                _lt.Position = env.UDim2.new(_Et, _Ct, _Ft, _Dt)
                _Bt = true
            end
        end
        if not _Bt then
            _lt.Position = _zt mobileButtonPositions[_gt] = nil
        end
        mobileButtonFrames[_gt] = {frame = _lt, defaultPosition = _zt, stroke = _tt, label = _wt, button = _yt, gradLayer = _qt, bgGrad = _rt,
        strokeGrad = _ut, flashLayer = _vt, btnSize = _kt, themeImg = _mt, themeIdx = _printedBtnSeq, ringStroke = _ot, ringGrad = _pt, panelX = _it,
        panelY = _jt,}
        local _Gt = false
        local _Ht, _It, _Jt, _Kt = nil, nil, nil, false
        local _Lt = 0
        local _Mt = 0
        local function _Nt()
            local _Ot = env.tick()
            if _Ot - _Mt < 0.18 then
                return
            end
            _Mt = _Ot _Lt = _Ot
            local _Pt = MobileButtonActions[_gt]
            if _Pt then
                env.task.spawn(function()
                    local _Qt, _Rt = env.pcall(_Pt)
                    if not _Qt then
                        env.warn("[Printed] mobile button " .. env.tostring(_gt) .. " failed: " .. env.tostring(_Rt))
                    end
                end
                )
            end
        end
        _yt.InputBegan:Connect(function(_St)
            if _St.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and _St.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                return
            end
            _Ht = _St.Position _It = _lt.Position _Jt = _at.Position _Kt = false _Lt = env.tick()
            if _qt then
                _qt.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
            end
            local _Tt = _lt.Size.X.Offset
            if _Tt < 1 then
                _Tt = 64
            end
            TweenService:Create(_lt, env.TweenInfo.new(0.08, env.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {Size = env.UDim2.new(0, _Tt - 4,
            0, _Tt - 4)}):Play()
            if _vt then
                _vt.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 TweenService:Create(_vt, env.TweenInfo.new(0.28, env.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, env.Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
            end
            if not uiLocked then
                _Gt = true
            end
        end
        ) _yt.InputEnded:Connect(function(_Ut)
            if not _Ht then
                return
            end
            _Ht = nil
            local _Vt = mobileButtonFrames[_gt] and mobileButtonFrames[_gt].btnSize or(env.tonumber(MOBILE_BTN_SIZE) or(235 % 0x3a + 61)) TweenService:Create(_lt,
            env.TweenInfo.new(0.15, env.Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, env.Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = env.UDim2.new(0,
            _Vt, 0, _Vt)}):Play() _Gt = false
            if _Kt then
                if _G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel == true then
                    mobileGroupPosition = {xs = _at.Position.X.Scale, x = _at.Position.X.Offset, ys = _at.Position.Y.Scale, y = _at.Position.Y.Offset}
                else
                    mobileButtonPositions[_gt] = {xs = _lt.Position.X.Scale, x = _lt.Position.X.Offset, ys = _lt.Position.Y.Scale, y = _lt.Position.Y.Offset}
                end
                if printedSavePositionsNow then
                    env.pcall(printedSavePositionsNow)
                else
                    _printedConfigDirty = true
                    if printedSaveConfig then
                        env.pcall(printedSaveConfig)
                    end
                end
            else
                _Nt()
            end
        end
        ) _yt.Activated:Connect(function()
            if not _Kt then
                _Nt()
            end
        end
        ) UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(_Wt)
            if uiLocked or not _Gt or not _Ht or not _It then
                return
            end
            if _Wt.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and _Wt.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                return
            end
            local _Xt = _Wt.Position.X - _Ht.X
            local _Yt = _Wt.Position.Y - _Ht.Y
            if env.math.abs(_Xt) > 5 or env.math.abs(_Yt) > 5 then
                _Kt = true
            end
            if not _Kt then
                return
            end
            if _G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel == true then
                if _Jt then
                    _at.Position = env.UDim2.new(_Jt.X.Scale, _Jt.X.Offset + _Xt, _Jt.Y.Scale, _Jt.Y.Offset + _Yt)
                end
                return
            end
            _lt.Position = env.UDim2.new(_It.X.Scale, _It.X.Offset + _Xt, _It.Y.Scale, _It.Y.Offset + _Yt)
        end
        )

        if PrintedTracer then
            PrintedTracer.enabled = false
        end
    end
    return _gw
end

local function _AB()
    local _BB = _printed_readfile or readfile or(syn and syn.readfile) or(getgenv and getgenv().readfile)
    local _CB = _printed_isfile or isfile or(syn and syn.isfile) or(getgenv and getgenv().isfile)
    if type(_BB) ~= "function" then
        return(1 ~= 1)
    end
    local _DB, _EB = env.pcall(function()
        if _CB and not _CB(PRINTED_CONFIG_FILE) then
            return nil
        end
        local raw = _BB(PRINTED_CONFIG_FILE)
        if not raw or raw == "" then
            return nil
        end
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw)
    end
    )
    if not _DB or type(_EB) ~= "table" then
        return(1 < 0)
    end
    local function _FB(_GB, _HB)
        local _IB = env.tonumber(_GB);
        return _IB or _HB
    end
    local function _JB(_KB, _LB)
        if _KB == nil then
            return _LB
        end
        return _KB and(2 > 1) or(2 < 1)
    end
    uiLocked = _JB(_EB.uiLocked, false)
    hideMobileButtons = _JB(_EB.hideMobileButtons, false) _G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel = _JB(_EB.mobileButtonsPanel, false)
    mobileButtonScaleValue = _FB(_EB.mobileButtonScaleValue or _EB.btnScale, 1.0) _G.PrintedBtnScale = mobileButtonScaleValue
    if type(_EB.mobileButtonPositions) == "table" then
        mobileButtonPositions = _EB.mobileButtonPositions
    end
    if type(_EB.mobileGroupPosition) == "table" then
        mobileGroupPosition = _EB.mobileGroupPosition
    end
    _G.PrintedGuiScale = _FB(_EB.guiScale, 0.82) _G.PrintedBarScale = _FB(_EB.barScale, 1) _G.PrintedAccentTheme = env.tostring(_EB.accentTheme or "Blue") _G.PrintedButtonMode = env.tostring(_EB.buttonMode or "Gradient") _G.PrintedBtnTheme = _FB(_EB.btnTheme,
    0) _G.__PrintedSavedStealPos = _EB.stealBarPosition _G.__PrintedSavedStealDragged = _EB.stealBarDragged == true _G.__PrintedSavedMainPos = _EB.mainGuiPosition _G.__PrintedSavedMiniPos = _EB.miniBtnPosition env.print("[Printed] early config loaded | lock=" .. env.tostring(uiLocked) .. " panel=" .. env.tostring(_G.PrintedMobileButtonsPanel) .. " btnScale=" .. env.tostring(mobileButtonScaleValue))
    return(2 > 1)
end
local _MB = false env.pcall(function()
    _MB = _AB()
end
)
if not _MB then
    env.print("[Printed] no early config / using defaults")
end

else

end

) UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(_kC, _lC)
    if _anyKeyListening then
        return
    end
    if _kC.UserInputType == env.Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
        if _lC or UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
            return
        end
    elseif _kC.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and _kC.UserInputType ~= env.Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2 then
        return
    end
    local _mC = _kC.KeyCode
    for _nC, _oC in env.ipairs(tbl18) do
        local _pC = tbl17[_oC.id]
        if _pC and(_pC.kb == _mC or _pC.gp == _mC) then
            local _qC = MobileButtonActions[_oC.id]
            if _qC then
                env.task.spawn(function()
                    env.pcall(_qC)
                end
                )
            end
            return
        end
    end
end
) env.task.spawn(function()
    local _rC = 0
    while(2 > 1) do
        env.task.wait(0.05)
        if not _printed_canSaveConfig then
            _printed_writefile = _printed_writefile or writefile or(syn and syn.writefile) _printed_canSaveConfig = type(_printed_writefile) == "function"
        end
        if _printed_canSaveConfig then
            if _printedConfigDirty then
                env.pcall(printedSaveConfig)
            elseif env.tick() - _rC > 1 then
                _rC = env.tick() env.pcall(printedSaveConfig)
            end
        end
    end
end
